[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Design a module named fp_mul_e5f10 that multiplies two floating-point numbers. Format: 16-bit (1 sign + 5 exponent + 10 fraction). Inputs: a and b (16-bit each). Output: y (16-bit). Subnormal numbers must be supported.

Required behaviour, bit-exact: subnormal inputs are used at their true value; results are rounded to nearest, ties to even, including results in the subnormal range; a NaN input and inf x 0 give the canonical quiet NaN 16'h7E00; inf x finite gives inf with the product's sign; an overflowing product gives inf; a zero product gives a signed zero. The testbench enumerates about 18.9 million operand pairs against a reference implementation.

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` in your workspace, a plain readable implementation (unpack, significand multiply, normalise, round, pack) that is correct. Optimize from there.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

[Step 1/60]
// fp_mul_e5f10 -- IEEE binary16 multiplier (1 sign, 5 exponent, 10 fraction bits) with subnormal support.

module fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);
  input  [15:0] a;
  input  [15:0] b;
  output [15:0] y;

  localparam BIAS         = 15;
  localparam MAX_E        = 31;   // inf / NaN
  localparam MAX_FINITE_E = 30;
  localparam PROD_W       = 22;   // 11 x 11 bit significand product

  // 1. unpack
  wire       s_a = a[15];
  wire       s_b = b[15];
  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];
  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];
  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];
  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];

  // 2. classify
  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);
  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);
  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == MAX_E);
  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == MAX_E);
  wire zero_a   = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);
  wire zero_b   = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);
  wire nan_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);
  wire nan_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);
  wire inf_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);
  wire inf_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);

  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);
  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;
  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;
  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;

  // 3. effective significands and exponents (subnormal: no hidden one, exponent 1)
  wire [10:0] m_a    = {~e_a_zero, f_a};
  wire [10:0] m_b    = {~e_b_zero, f_b};
  wire [4:0]  ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a;
  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;

  // 4. significand product, its leading-zero count (0 for a zero product), below[k] = |prod[k:0]
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;

  reg [4:0] lz;
  integer   i;
  always @* begin
    lz = 5'd0;
    for (i = 0; i < PROD_W; i = i + 1)
      if (prod[i]) lz = 5'd21 - i[4:0];
  end

  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;
  genvar k;
  generate
    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below
      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];
    end
  endgenerate

  function bit_at;   // v[idx], 0 outside the vector
    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;
    input integer      idx;
    begin
      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? v[idx] : 1'b0;
    end
  endfunction

  // 5. normalise and round a normal result: leading one onto bit 10
  wire              need_right = (lz <= 5'd11);
  wire [4:0]        sr         = 5'd11 - lz;   // right shift (wide product)
  wire [4:0]        sl         = lz - 5'd11;   // left shift (narrow product)
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted    = need_right ? (prod >> sr) : (prod << sl);
  wire [10:0]       mant_pre   = shifted[10:0];

  wire guard    = need_right ? bit_at(prod,  sr - 1) : 1'b0;   // first bit shifted out
  wire sticky   = need_right ? bit_at(below, sr - 2) : 1'b0;   // any bit below the guard
  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);              // round to nearest, ties to even

  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};
  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];   // rounded up to a new leading one
  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];
  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];

  // 6. exponent of a normal result
  wire [7:0] exp_sum     = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};
  wire [7:0] lhs         = exp_sum + 8'd1 + {7'd0, carry};
  wire [7:0] limit_under = BIAS + {3'd0, lz};
  wire [7:0] limit_over  = BIAS + MAX_FINITE_E + {3'd0, lz};
  wire       underflow   = (lhs <= limit_under);   // subnormal (or zero) result
  wire       overflow    = (lhs >  limit_over);    // past the largest finite number
  wire [7:0] e_norm      = lhs - limit_under;
  wire [4:0] exp_norm    = e_norm[4:0];

  // 7. subnormal result: shift the raw product onto the subnormal grid, round once
  integer           total_shift;
  reg [PROD_W-1:0]  sub_shifted;
  always @* begin
    total_shift = (BIAS + 10 + 1) - $signed({1'b0, exp_sum});
    sub_shifted = (total_shift >= 0 && total_shift < PROD_W) ? (prod >> total_shift) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};
  end
  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = sub_shifted[9:0];
  wire        guard_s    = bit_at(prod,  total_shift - 1);
  wire        sticky_s   = bit_at(below, total_shift - 2);
  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);
  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};
  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];   // rounded up to the smallest normal
  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];
  wire [4:0]  exp_sub    = {4'd0, carry_s};

  // 8. pack
  wire is_nan      = nan_in;
  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));
  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;
  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & (exp_sub == 5'd0) & (frac_sub == 10'd0);
  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);

  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? exp_sub : exp_norm;
  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;
  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;

  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};
endmodule

[Step 2/60]
`timescale 1ns/10ps
// exhaustive-directed tb (fpmul_run)
// Golden-referenced directed testbench: compares the DUT against the embedded
// golden reference (fp_mul_e5f10_ref, generated from context/starting_point.py)
// over ~18.9M enumerated vectors — no stimuli file. Structure:
//   1. the 3 known subnormal-boundary regression pairs (+ golden self-checks)
//   2. every subnormal/zero a (2^11 sign+mantissa patterns) x 4096 structured b
//   3. 4096 structured a x every subnormal/zero b
//   4. 2^21 LFSR-driven broad pairs
// Structured operands: both signs x all 32 exponents x 64 mantissas
// (8 corners + 56 LFSR-derived). The retired file-driven testbench is kept at
// tb_vectors.sv (+ vectors.dat) for cross-checking; it is not compiled.
module tb;
  logic [15:0] a, b, y, y_ref;
  int errors;
  longint total;

  fp_mul_e5f10 dut (.a(a), .b(b), .y(y));
  fp_mul_e5f10_ref refm (.a(a), .b(b), .y(y_ref));

  function automatic [15:0] lfsr_next(input [15:0] s);
    lfsr_next = (s >> 1) ^ (s[0] ? 16'hB400 : 16'h0000);
  endfunction

  logic [15:0] structured [0:4095];
  localparam logic [9:0] MANT_CORNERS [0:7] =
      '{10'h000, 10'h001, 10'h002, 10'h003, 10'h3FF, 10'h3FE, 10'h2AA, 10'h155};

  task automatic check();
    #1;
    total = total + 1;
    if (y !== y_ref) begin
      if (errors < 10)
        $display("TB_ERROR a=%0d b=%0d expected_y=%0d actual_y=%0d", a, b, y_ref, y);
      errors = errors + 1;
    end
  endtask

  logic [15:0] lfsr, ra, rb;
  logic [9:0] mant;
  int idx;

  initial begin
    errors = 0; total = 0;

    lfsr = 16'hACE1;
    idx = 0;
    for (int s = 0; s < 2; s++)
      for (int e = 0; e < 32; e++)
        for (int m = 0; m < 64; m++) begin
          if (m < 8) mant = MANT_CORNERS[m];
          else begin lfsr = lfsr_next(lfsr); mant = lfsr[9:0]; end
          structured[idx] = {s[0], e[4:0], mant};
          idx = idx + 1;
        end

    a = 16'h0003; b = 16'h6801; check();
    if (y_ref !== 16'h0E02) begin errors++; $display("TB_ERROR golden self-check 1"); end
    a = 16'h8003; b = 16'h6479; check();
    if (y_ref !== 16'h8AB6) begin errors++; $display("TB_ERROR golden self-check 2"); end
    a = 16'h7521; b = 16'h0003; check();
    if (y_ref !== 16'h1BB2) begin errors++; $display("TB_ERROR golden self-check 3"); end

    for (int sa = 0; sa < 2048; sa++) begin
      a = {sa[10], 5'b00000, sa[9:0]};
      for (int i = 0; i < 4096; i++) begin b = structured[i]; check(); end
    end
    for (int i = 0; i < 4096; i++) begin
      a = structured[i];
      for (int sb = 0; sb < 2048; sb++) begin b = {sb[10], 5'b00000, sb[9:0]}; check(); end
    end
    ra = 16'h0001; rb = 16'hBEEF;
    for (int k = 0; k < (1 << 21); k++) begin
      ra = lfsr_next(ra); rb = lfsr_next(lfsr_next(rb));
      a = ra; b = rb; check();
    end

    $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total, errors);
    if (errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");
    $display("PASS");
    $finish;
  end
endmodule

[ASSISTANT]
I've reviewed the starting point and testbench. My plan:

1. **Baseline**: Evaluate `starting_point.v` to get the reference cost.
2. **v2 architecture**: Redesign with input-side subnormal normalization (small 10-bit LZC + shift per operand), so the 22-bit product has leading zero count ∈ {0,1} — eliminating the 22-bit LZC and large barrel shifters of the starting point. Single rounding, with a small 13-bit subnormal right-shift path.

[ASSISTANT]
The baseline passed, with a cost of **145429.2** (area 108.7, delay 1338ps). The critical path runs through classification → 22-bit LZC → 22-bit barrel shift → rounding, and is very deep.

**Optimization plan (v2):** Normalize subnormal operands at the *input* (per-operand 10-bit LZC + small left shift). This makes the 22-bit product of two normalized significands have at most 1 leading zero (just `p[21]`). This eliminates the 22-bit LZC and the large bidirectional shifter. The subnormal-result path becomes a small staged funnel shifter that computes the sticky bit in parallel. Let me work through the arithmetic carefully:

- For subnormals, `ma = {fa,1'b0} << lzc(fa)`, with `ea_e = -lzc`; otherwise `{1'b1, fa}`, with `ea_e = ea`
- `p = ma*mb`, `mant = p[p[21]] ? p>>1 : p` (only a 1-bit normalization, leading 1 is at bit 20)
- `t = ea_e + eb_e + p[21]`; normal exponent = `t-15`; subnormal ⇔ `t ≤ 15`; overflow ⇔ `t ≥ 46` or (`t==45` and rounding carry)
- Subnormal: shift window = 15−t over `mant[20:10]`, single rounding, carry → smallest normal


  -> create_file(filename='v2.v', content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v2 -- input-side subnormal normalization.\n// Subnormals are normalized before the multiply, so the 22-bit product has at\n// most one leading zero (p[21]); no wide LZC or wide barrel shifter is needed.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- unpack ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire ez_a = ~|ea, ez_b = ~|eb;          // exponent field zero\n  wire eo_a = &ea,  eo_b = &eb;           // exponent field all ones\n  wire fz_a = ~|fa, fz_b = ~|fb;          // fraction zero\n  wire zero_a = ez_a & fz_a,  zero_b = ez_b & fz_b;\n  wire inf_a  = eo_a & fz_a,  inf_b  = eo_b & fz_b;\n  wire nan_a  = eo_a & ~fz_a, nan_b  = eo_b & ~fz_b;\n  wire sub_a  = ez_a & ~fz_a, sub_b  = ez_b & ~fz_b;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count of a 10-bit fraction ----\n  function automatic [3:0] lzc10;\n    input [9:0] x;\n    begin\n      lzc10 = x[9] ? 4'd0 : x[8] ? 4'd1 : x[7] ? 4'd2 : x[6] ? 4'd3 :\n              x[5] ? 4'd4 : x[4] ? 4'd5 : x[3] ? 4'd6 : x[2] ? 4'd7 :\n              x[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  wire [3:0] lz_a = lzc10(fa);\n  wire [3:0] lz_b = lzc10(fb);\n\n  // ---- normalized significands (leading 1 at bit 10) + effective biased exp ----\n  wire [10:0] ma = sub_a ? ({fa, 1'b0} << lz_a) : {1'b1, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb = sub_b ? ({fb, 1'b0} << lz_b) : {1'b1, fb};\n\n  wire signed [6:0] ea_e = sub_a ? -$signed({3'b000, lz_a}) : $signed({2'b00, ea});\n  wire signed [6:0] eb_e = sub_b ? -$signed({3'b000, lz_b}) : $signed({2'b00, eb});\n\n  // ---- multiply ----\n  wire [21:0] p    = ma * mb;\n  wire        p_hi = p[21];\n  wire [20:0] mant = p_hi ? p[21:1] : p[20:0];   // leading 1 at bit 20\n\n  wire signed [6:0] t = ea_e + eb_e + {6'd0, p_hi};\n\n  wire        is_sub  = (t <= 7'sd15);   // E = t-15 <= 0 -> subnormal grid\n  wire        ovf_pre = (t >= 7'sd46);\n  wire        t_eq45  = (t == 7'sd45);\n\n  // ---- subnormal result path: staged right shifter with sticky in parallel ----\n  // sel = 15 - t  (= -E), clamped; result is zero once sel >= 11.\n  wire [5:0] sel6 = 6'd15 - t[5:0];\n  wire [4:0] sel  = (sel6 > 6'd15) ? 5'd15 : sel6[4:0];\n\n  wire [10:0] e0 = mant[20:10];\n  wire [10:0] e1 = sel[0] ? (e0 >> 1) : e0;\n  wire        o1 = sel[0] & e0[0];\n  wire [10:0] e2 = sel[1] ? (e1 >> 2) : e1;\n  wire        o2 = sel[1] & (|e1[1:0]);\n  wire [10:0] e4 = sel[2] ? (e2 >> 4) : e2;\n  wire        o4 = sel[2] & (|e2[3:0]);\n  wire [10:0] e8 = sel[3] ? (e4 >> 8) : e4;\n  wire        o8 = sel[3] & (|e4[7:0]);\n\n  wire [9:0] frac_s  = e8[10:1];\n  wire       guard_s = e8[0];\n  wire       sticky_s = (|mant[9:0]) | o1 | o2 | o4 | o8;\n\n  // ---- normal result path fields ----\n  wire [9:0] frac_n  = mant[19:10];\n  wire       guard_n = mant[9];\n  wire       sticky_n = |mant[8:0];\n\n  // ---- shared rounding (round to nearest, ties to even) ----\n  wire [9:0] frac_pre = is_sub ? frac_s  : frac_n;\n  wire       guard    = is_sub ? guard_s : guard_n;\n  wire       sticky   = is_sub ? sticky_s : sticky_n;\n  wire       round_up = guard & (sticky | frac_pre[0]);\n\n  wire [10:0] frac11  = {1'b0, frac_pre} + {10'd0, round_up};\n  wire        carry   = frac11[10];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_o  = frac11[9:0];\n\n  // ---- final exponent / flags ----\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm = t[4:0] - 5'd15 + {4'd0, carry};\n  wire [4:0] exp_o    = is_sub ? {4'd0, carry} : exp_norm;\n  wire       ovf      = ovf_pre | (t_eq45 & carry);\n\n  // ---- pack ----\n  wire [15:0] core = ovf ? {sy, 5'h1F, 10'd0} : {sy, exp_o, frac_o};\n\n  assign y = nan_in  ? 16'h7E00 :\n             inf_in  ? {sy, 5'h1F, 10'd0} :\n             zero_in ? {sy, 15'd0} :\n             core;\nendmodule\n")

[ASSISTANT]
Found the bug: when `p[21]=1`, I'm right-shifting the product to obtain `mant`, and **the bit of `p[0]` is being dropped from the sticky**. In both paths, the sticky must include `p[0]` when `p_hi=1`. I'll fix it:


  -> edit_file(filename='v2.v', old_string='  wire [9:0] frac_s  = e8[10:1];\n  wire       guard_s = e8[0];\n  wire       sticky_s = (|mant[9:0]) | o1 | o2 | o4 | o8;\n\n  // ---- normal result path fields ----\n  wire [9:0] frac_n  = mant[19:10];\n  wire       guard_n = mant[9];\n  wire       sticky_n = |mant[8:0];', new_string="  // p[0] is dropped from 'mant' when p_hi is set; it must still feed the sticky\n  wire p0 = p_hi & p[0];\n\n  wire [9:0] frac_s  = e8[10:1];\n  wire       guard_s = e8[0];\n  wire       sticky_s = (|mant[9:0]) | o1 | o2 | o4 | o8 | p0;\n\n  // ---- normal result path fields ----\n  wire [9:0] frac_n  = mant[19:10];\n  wire       guard_n = mant[9];\n  wire       sticky_n = (|mant[8:0]) | p0;")

  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v3 -- input-side subnormal normalization + fast exponent path.\n// t = ea+eb (early, from yosys sum) + lz + lz + hi, computed once; subnormal\n// result exponent is just t[0], so the packing mux is shallow.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- unpack ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  // ---- classify (fast, parallel) ----\n  wire ez_a = ~|ea, ez_b = ~|eb;          // exponent field zero\n  wire eo_a = &ea,  eo_b = &eb;           // exponent field all ones\n  wire fz_a = ~|fa, fz_b = ~|fb;          // fraction zero\n  wire zero_a = ez_a & fz_a,  zero_b = ez_b & fz_b;\n  wire inf_a  = eo_a & fz_a,  inf_b  = eo_b & fz_b;\n  wire nan_a  = eo_a & ~fz_a, nan_b  = eo_b & ~fz_b;\n  wire sub_a  = ez_a & ~fz_a, sub_b  = ez_b & ~fz_b;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count of the 10-bit fractions (encoded 1-out-of-10) ----\n  wire [3:0] lz_a, lz_b;\n  assign lz_a = fa[9] ? 4'd0 : fa[8] ? 4'd1 : fa[7] ? 4'd2 : fa[6] ? 4'd3 :\n                fa[5] ? 4'd4 : fa[4] ? 4'd5 : fa[3] ? 4'd6 : fa[2] ? 4'd7 :\n                fa[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n  assign lz_b = fb[9] ? 4'd0 : fb[8] ? 4'd1 : fb[7] ? 4'd2 : fb[6] ? 4'd3 :\n                fb[5] ? 4'd4 : fb[4] ? 4'd5 : fb[3] ? 4'd6 : fb[2] ? 4'd7 :\n                fb[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n  wire fb_lsb = fb[1] | fb[0];   // 10th encoded operand for t\n\n  // ---- normalized significands (leading 1 at bit 10) ----\n  wire [10:0] ma = sub_a ? ({fa, 1'b0} << lz_a) : {1'b1, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb = sub_b ? ({fb, 1'b0} << lz_b) : {1'b1, fb};\n\n  // ---- early exponent sum: t = ea + eb + lz_a + lz_b + p_hi ----\n  // ea+eb (yosys adder) + (lz_a + lz_b + fb_lsb) (yosys adder) in parallel.\n  wire [5:0] t0 = {1'b0, ea} + {1'b0, eb};\n  wire [4:0] lzs = {1'b0, lz_a} + {lz_b, fb_lsb};\n  wire [21:0] p    = ma * mb;\n  wire        p_hi = p[21];\n  wire [20:0] mant = p_hi ? p[21:1] : p[20:0];   // leading 1 at bit 20\n\n  wire [6:0] tsum = {1'b0, t0} + {2'b00, lzs};\n  wire [6:0] t    = tsum + {6'd0, p_hi};\n\n  wire        is_sub  = ~t[6] & ~t[5] & (t[4:0] <= 5'd15); // t <= 15 -> subnormal\n  wire        ovf_pre = t[5] | (t[6] & t[5] == 1'b0 & 1'b0) ; // placeholder, replaced below\n\n  // overflow: t >= 46 (t cannot exceed 30+30+19+19+1 = 99 < 128, so t[6]=0 region)\n  wire        ovf0    = t[5] | (&t[4:0]);                    // t >= 32 or t==31.. check below\n  // proper overflow: t>=46 -> since t<64 here, t[5] && t[4:0]>=14 -> use full compare\n  wire        ovf_cmp = ~t[6] & (t[5:0] >= 6'd46);\n\n  wire        t_eq45  = (t[5:0] == 6'd45) & ~t[6];\n\n  // ---- subnormal result path: staged right shifter with sticky in parallel ----\n  wire [5:0] sel6 = 6'd15 - t[5:0];\n  wire [4:0] sel  = (sel6 > 6'd15) ? 5'd15 : sel6[4:0];\n\n  wire [10:0] e0 = mant[20:10];\n  wire [10:0] e1 = sel[0] ? (e0 >> 1) : e0;\n  wire        o1 = sel[0] & e0[0];\n  wire [10:0] e2 = sel[1] ? (e1 >> 2) : e1;\n  wire        o2 = sel[1] & (|e1[1:0]);\n  wire [10:0] e4 = sel[2] ? (e2 >> 4) : e2;\n  wire        o4 = sel[2] & (|e2[3:0]);\n  wire [10:0] e8 = sel[3] ? (e4 >> 8) : e4;\n  wire        o8 = sel[3] & (|e4[7:0]);\n\n  wire p0 = p_hi & p[0];   // dropped LSB still feeds the sticky\n\n  wire [9:0] frac_s  = e8[10:1];\n  wire       guard_s = e8[0];\n  wire       sticky_s = (|mant[9:0]) | o1 | o2 | o4 | o8 | p0;\n\n  // ---- normal result path fields ----\n  wire [9:0] frac_n  = mant[19:10];\n  wire       guard_n = mant[9];\n  wire       sticky_n = (|mant[8:0]) | p0;\n\n  // ---- shared rounding ----\n  wire [9:0] frac_pre = is_sub ? frac_s  : frac_n;\n  wire       guard    = is_sub ? guard_s : guard_n;\n  wire       sticky   = is_sub ? sticky_s : sticky_n;\n  wire       round_up = guard & (sticky | frac_pre[0]);\n\n  wire [10:0] frac11  = {1'b0, frac_pre} + {10'd0, round_up};\n  wire        carry   = frac11[10];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_o  = frac11[9:0];\n\n  // ---- final exponent / flags ----\n  wire [4:0] exp_n    = t[4:0] - 5'd15 + {4'd0, carry};\n  wire [4:0] exp_o    = is_sub ? {4'd0, t[0] & carry} : exp_n;\n  wire       ovf      = ovf_cmp | (t_eq45 & carry);\n\n  // ---- pack ----\n  wire [15:0] core = ovf ? {sy, 5'h1F, 10'd0} : {sy, exp_o, frac_o};\n\n  assign y = nan_in  ? 16'h7E00 :\n             inf_in  ? {sy, 5'h1F, 10'd0} :\n             zero_in ? {sy, 15'd0} :\n             core;\nendmodule\n", filename='v3.v')

[ASSISTANT]
Target delay has no effect (synthesis result identical), so RTL structure is the real lever. Looking at the critical path tail (~290ps of packing/mux logic after the rounding adder), I'll flatten the final packing mux into bitwise AND-gating so fraction bits become just `frac_o & not_special` instead of a 3-level mux chain. Let me create v5:


  -> create_file(content='// fp_mul_e5f10 v5 -- v4 structure + flattened bit-gated packing.\n// Fraction bits are gated with an early "not special" signal instead of\n// passing through a 3-level nan/inf/zero/core mux chain.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- unpack ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire ez_a = ~|ea, ez_b = ~|eb;\n  wire eo_a = &ea,  eo_b = &eb;\n  wire fz_a = ~|fa, fz_b = ~|fb;\n  wire zero_a = ez_a & fz_a,  zero_b = ez_b & fz_b;\n  wire inf_a  = eo_a & fz_a,  inf_b  = eo_b & fz_b;\n  wire nan_a  = eo_a & ~fz_a, nan_b  = eo_b & ~fz_b;\n  wire sub_a  = ez_a & ~fz_a, sub_b  = ez_b & ~fz_b;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count of the 10-bit fractions ----\n  wire [3:0] lz_a = fa[9] ? 4\'d0 : fa[8] ? 4\'d1 : fa[7] ? 4\'d2 : fa[6] ? 4\'d3 :\n                    fa[5] ? 4\'d4 : fa[4] ? 4\'d5 : fa[3] ? 4\'d6 : fa[2] ? 4\'d7 :\n                    fa[1] ? 4\'d8 : 4\'d9;\n  wire [3:0] lz_b = fb[9] ? 4\'d0 : fb[8] ? 4\'d1 : fb[7] ? 4\'d2 : fb[6] ? 4\'d3 :\n                    fb[5] ? 4\'d4 : fb[4] ? 4\'d5 : fb[3] ? 4\'d6 : fb[2] ? 4\'d7 :\n                    fb[1] ? 4\'d8 : 4\'d9;\n\n  // ---- normalized significands (leading 1 at bit 10) ----\n  wire [10:0] ma = sub_a ? ({fa, 1\'b0} << lz_a) : {1\'b1, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb = sub_b ? ({fb, 1\'b0} << lz_b) : {1\'b1, fb};\n\n  // ---- exponent sums ----\n  wire signed [6:0] ea_e = sub_a ? -$signed({3\'b000, lz_a}) : $signed({2\'b00, ea});\n  wire signed [6:0] eb_e = sub_b ? -$signed({3\'b000, lz_b}) : $signed({2\'b00, eb});\n  wire signed [6:0] t_base = ea_e + eb_e;\n\n  // ---- multiply ----\n  wire [21:0] p    = ma * mb;\n  wire        p_hi = p[21];\n  wire [20:0] mant = p_hi ? p[21:1] : p[20:0];\n\n  wire signed [6:0] t = t_base + {6\'d0, p_hi};\n  wire        is_sub  = (t <= 7\'sd15);\n  wire        ovf_cmp = (t >= 7\'sd46);\n  wire        t_eq45  = (t == 7\'sd45);\n\n  // ---- subnormal result path (p_hi-independent shift) ----\n  wire signed [7:0] R_full = 8\'sd15 - t_base;\n  wire [3:0] R = (R_full >= 8\'sd15) ? 4\'d15 :\n                 (R_full <  8\'sd0)  ? 4\'d0  : R_full[3:0];\n\n  wire [11:0] e0 = p[21:10];\n  wire [11:0] e1 = R[0] ? (e0 >> 1) : e0;\n  wire        o1 = R[0] & e0[0];\n  wire [11:0] e2 = R[1] ? (e1 >> 2) : e1;\n  wire        o2 = R[1] & (|e1[1:0]);\n  wire [11:0] e4 = R[2] ? (e2 >> 4) : e2;\n  wire        o4 = R[2] & (|e2[3:0]);\n  wire [11:0] e8 = R[3] ? (e4 >> 8) : e4;\n  wire        o8 = R[3] & (|e4[7:0]);\n\n  wire [9:0] frac_s  = e8[10:1];\n  wire       guard_s = e8[0];\n  wire       sticky_s = (|p[9:0]) | o1 | o2 | o4 | o8;\n\n  // ---- normal result path fields ----\n  wire p0 = p_hi & p[0];\n  wire [9:0] frac_n  = mant[19:10];\n  wire       guard_n = mant[9];\n  wire       sticky_n = (|mant[8:0]) | p0;\n\n  // ---- shared rounding ----\n  wire [9:0] frac_pre = is_sub ? frac_s  : frac_n;\n  wire       guard    = is_sub ? guard_s : guard_n;\n  wire       sticky   = is_sub ? sticky_s : sticky_n;\n  wire       round_up = guard & (sticky | frac_pre[0]);\n\n  wire [10:0] frac11  = {1\'b0, frac_pre} + {10\'d0, round_up};\n  wire        carry   = frac11[10];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_o  = frac11[9:0];\n\n  // ---- final exponent / flags ----\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm = t[4:0] - 5\'d15 + {4\'d0, carry};\n  wire [4:0] exp_o    = is_sub ? {4\'d0, carry} : exp_norm;\n  wire       ovf      = ovf_cmp | (t_eq45 & carry);\n\n  // ---- flattened packing ----\n  wire [9:0] frac_core = ovf ? 10\'d0 : frac_o;\n  wire [4:0] exp_core  = ovf ? 5\'d31 : exp_o;\n\n  wire not_special = ~nan_in & ~inf_in & ~zero_in;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y;\n  assign frac_y[8:0] = frac_core[8:0] & {9{not_special}};\n  assign frac_y[9]   = nan_in | (frac_core[9] & not_special);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y = (nan_in | inf_in) ? 5\'d31 :\n                     zero_in ? 5\'d0 : exp_core;\n\n  wire sign_y = sy & ~nan_in;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n', filename='v5.v')

[ASSISTANT]
  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v6 -- folded subnormal normalization.\n// A subnormal operand fa.2^-10 is ma = fa' << lz  (fa' in [2^9,2^10)).\n// Writing lz = 10*k + r (k in {0,1}, r in 0..9) and folding the word shift k\n// into the partial products (one 3:2 mux per product row) leaves a single\n// 9..10-bit * 10-bit multiply per side. Exponents use one-hot LZC encodings.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- unpack ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire ez_a = ~|ea, ez_b = ~|eb;\n  wire eo_a = &ea,  eo_b = &eb;\n  wire fz_a = ~|fa, fz_b = ~|fb;\n  wire zero_a = ez_a & fz_a,  zero_b = ez_b & fz_b;\n  wire inf_a  = eo_a & fz_a,  inf_b  = eo_b & fz_b;\n  wire nan_a  = eo_a & ~fz_a, nan_b  = eo_b & ~fz_b;\n  wire sub_a  = ez_a & ~fz_a, sub_b  = ez_b & ~fz_b;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- one-hot LZC of the 10-bit fractions: lz1[i] = (lzc == i) ----\n  wire [9:0] lz_a, lz_b;\n  genvar i;\n  generate\n    for (i = 9; i >= 0; i = i - 1) begin : glz\n      assign lz_a[i] = fa[i] & (i == 9 ? 1'b1 : ~|fa[9:i+1]);\n      assign lz_b[i] = fb[i] & (i == 9 ? 1'b1 : ~|fb[9:i+1]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  // ---- effective biased exponents (one-hot arithmetic, no 4-bit code) ----\n  // normal: ea  ; subnormal: -lz  (only one lz_a bit can be set)\n  wire [4:0] ea_e = (sub_a & (|{lz_a[9],lz_a[8],lz_a[7],lz_a[6],lz_a[5]})) ?\n                    5'd0 : {2'b00, ea[2:0]};\n  // more explicit: t components as 6-bit signed words:\n  // ea_e = sub_a ? -lz : ea  encoded via an adder input per one-hot bit.\n\n  // ---- residual (r = lz mod 10) normalized fractions ----\n  wire [9:0] fa_n = sub_a ? ((fa << lz_a[0]) | (fa << 2*lz_a[1]) |\n                             (fa << 3*lz_a[2]) | (fa << 4*lz_a[3]) |\n                             (fa << 5*lz_a[4]) | (fa << 6*lz_a[5]) |\n                             (fa << 7*lz_a[6]) | (fa << 8*lz_a[7]) |\n                             (fa << 9*lz_a[8]) | (fa << 10*lz_a[9]))\n                          : fa;\n  wire [9:0] fb_n = sub_b ? ((fb << lz_b[0]) | (fb << 2*lz_b[1]) |\n                             (fb << 3*lz_b[2]) | (fb << 4*lz_b[3]) |\n                             (fb << 5*lz_b[4]) | (fb << 6*lz_b[5]) |\n                             (fb << 7*lz_b[6]) | (fb << 8*lz_b[7]) |\n                             (fb << 9*lz_b[8]) | (fb << 10*lz_b[9]))\n                          : fb;\n\n  // ---- partial products with folded 10-bit word shift ----\n  wire k_a = sub_a & (lz_a[6] | lz_a[7] | lz_a[8] | lz_a[9]); // lz >= 6? no: word shift is lz/10\n  // word shift: fa' = fa << lz, split as (fa << r) << 10k. r = lz - 10k.\n  // Since lz in 0..9 normally (r=lz,k=0) but fa already covers bit10 via the\n  // hidden shift: fa' in [2^9,2^10) sits in bits [9:0]; the word shift k moves\n  // it to [19:10] (k=1, lz in 10..18 impossible, lz<=9). So k is always 0.\n\n  // Thus the folded multiplier is simply fa_n (10 bits, leading 1 at bit 9)\n  // with an implicit x2^1 (bit-10 hidden one) -> ma = {1'b0?} handled below.\n\n  // For uniformity: mantissand_A = {hn_a, fa_n} with hn_a = hidden bit at 10:\n  //   normal  -> hn_a = 1, fa_n = fa\n  //   subnormal -> hn_a = 0, fa_n = fa << lz  (leading 1 somewhere in [9:0])\n  wire [10:0] ma = sub_a ? {1'b0, fa_n} : {1'b1, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb = sub_b ? {1'b0, fb_n} : {1'b1, fb};\n\n  // ---- exponent sum components ----\n  // t = (ea_eff + eb_eff) + p_hi,  ea_eff = sub ? (1 - lz) : ea  ...\n  // Use the bit-10 view: ma = fa_eff.2^(e_eff?) ... keep v4 formulation:\n  // subnormal true significand 0.fa * 2^1 -> after normalization leading one\n  // at bit 10 with value ma * 2^(1 - 10 - lz)... simplest: reuse v4 adders.\n  wire [3:0] lz_a_code = lz_a[0] ? 4'd0 : lz_a[1] ? 4'd1 : lz_a[2] ? 4'd2 :\n                         lz_a[3] ? 4'd3 : lz_a[4] ? 4'd4 : lz_a[5] ? 4'd5 :\n                         lz_a[6] ? 4'd6 : lz_a[7] ? 4'd7 : lz_a[8] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n  wire [3:0] lz_b_code = lz_b[0] ? 4'd0 : lz_b[1] ? 4'd1 : lz_b[2] ? 4'd2 :\n                         lz_b[3] ? 4'd3 : lz_b[4] ? 4'd4 : lz_b[5] ? 4'd5 :\n                         lz_b[6] ? 4'd6 : lz_b[7] ? 4'd7 : lz_b[8] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n\n  wire signed [6:0] ea_e2 = sub_a ? -$signed({3'b000, lz_a_code}) : $signed({2'b00, ea});\n  wire signed [6:0] eb_e2 = sub_b ? -$signed({3'b000, lz_b_code}) : $signed({2'b00, eb});\n  wire signed [6:0] t_base = ea_e2 + eb_e2;\n\n  // ---- multiply ----\n  wire [21:0] p    = ma * mb;\n  wire        p_hi = p[21];\n  wire [20:0] mant = p_hi ? p[21:1] : p[20:0];\n\n  wire signed [6:0] t = t_base + {6'd0, p_hi};\n  wire        is_sub  = (t <= 7'sd15);\n  wire        ovf_cmp = (t >= 7'sd46);\n  wire        t_eq45  = (t == 7'sd45);\n\n  // ---- subnormal result path ----\n  wire signed [7:0] R_full = 8'sd15 - t_base;\n  wire [3:0] R = (R_full >= 8'sd15) ? 4'd15 :\n                 (R_full <  8'sd0)  ? 4'd0  : R_full[3:0];\n\n  wire [11:0] e0 = p[21:10];\n  wire [11:0] e1 = R[0] ? (e0 >> 1) : e0;\n  wire        o1 = R[0] & e0[0];\n  wire [11:0] e2 = R[1] ? (e1 >> 2) : e1;\n  wire        o2 = R[1] & (|e1[1:0]);\n  wire [11:0] e4 = R[2] ? (e2 >> 4) : e2;\n  wire        o4 = R[2] & (|e2[3:0]);\n  wire [11:0] e8 = R[3] ? (e4 >> 8) : e4;\n  wire        o8 = R[3] & (|e4[7:0]);\n\n  wire [9:0] frac_s  = e8[10:1];\n  wire       guard_s = e8[0];\n  wire       sticky_s = (|p[9:0]) | o1 | o2 | o4 | o8;\n\n  // ---- normal result path fields ----\n  wire p0 = p_hi & p[0];\n  wire [9:0] frac_n  = mant[19:10];\n  wire       guard_n = mant[9];\n  wire       sticky_n = (|mant[8:0]) | p0;\n\n  // ---- shared rounding ----\n  wire [9:0] frac_pre = is_sub ? frac_s  : frac_n;\n  wire       guard    = is_sub ? guard_s : guard_n;\n  wire       sticky   = is_sub ? sticky_s : sticky_n;\n  wire       round_up = guard & (sticky | frac_pre[0]);\n\n  wire [10:0] frac11  = {1'b0, frac_pre} + {10'd0, round_up};\n  wire        carry   = frac11[10];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_o  = frac11[9:0];\n\n  // ---- final exponent / flags ----\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm = t[4:0] - 5'd15 + {4'd0, carry};\n  wire [4:0] exp_o    = is_sub ? {4'd0, carry} : exp_norm;\n  wire       ovf      = ovf_cmp | (t_eq45 & carry);\n\n  // ---- flattened packing ----\n  wire [9:0] frac_core = ovf ? 10'd0 : frac_o;\n  wire [4:0] exp_core  = ovf ? 5'd31 : exp_o;\n\n  wire not_special = ~nan_in & ~inf_in & ~zero_in;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y;\n  assign frac_y[8:0] = frac_core[8:0] & {9{not_special}};\n  assign frac_y[9]   = nan_in | (frac_core[9] & not_special);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y = (nan_in | inf_in) ? 5'd31 :\n                     zero_in ? 5'd0 : exp_core;\n\n  wire sign_y = sy & ~nan_in;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='v6.v')

  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v7 -- v5 structure, retimed tail:\n//  * t_base comparisons precomputed early; p_hi only selects between them\n//  * frac/guard/sticky muxed straight from p (no intermediate 'mant')\n//  * rounding increment computed in parallel with round_up (mux, not adder,\n//    after round_up); carry = all-ones & round_up\n//  * exp_norm = (t_base-15) + p_hi + carry via precomputed table + small mux\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- unpack ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire ez_a = ~|ea, ez_b = ~|eb;\n  wire eo_a = &ea,  eo_b = &eb;\n  wire fz_a = ~|fa, fz_b = ~|fb;\n  wire zero_a = ez_a & fz_a,  zero_b = ez_b & fz_b;\n  wire inf_a  = eo_a & fz_a,  inf_b  = eo_b & fz_b;\n  wire nan_a  = eo_a & ~fz_a, nan_b  = eo_b & ~fz_b;\n  wire sub_a  = ez_a & ~fz_a, sub_b  = ez_b & ~fz_b;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count of the 10-bit fractions ----\n  wire [3:0] lz_a = fa[9] ? 4'd0 : fa[8] ? 4'd1 : fa[7] ? 4'd2 : fa[6] ? 4'd3 :\n                    fa[5] ? 4'd4 : fa[4] ? 4'd5 : fa[3] ? 4'd6 : fa[2] ? 4'd7 :\n                    fa[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n  wire [3:0] lz_b = fb[9] ? 4'd0 : fb[8] ? 4'd1 : fb[7] ? 4'd2 : fb[6] ? 4'd3 :\n                    fb[5] ? 4'd4 : fb[4] ? 4'd5 : fb[3] ? 4'd6 : fb[2] ? 4'd7 :\n                    fb[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n\n  // ---- normalized significands (leading 1 at bit 10) ----\n  wire [10:0] ma = sub_a ? [ASSISTANT]
v7 passes at 110836. The tail after the multiplier is: funnel shifter → round_up → 10-bit ripple increment → packing. Key insight: the rounding increment `frac_o = frac_pre + round_up` only ripples when frac_pre has trailing 1s — `cin[i] = round_up & &frac_pre[i-1:0]`. So I can precompute prefix-AND of frac_pre in parallel with the shifter, reducing the round_up→output path to one XOR. I'll also compute normal and subnormal rounding fully in parallel. Let me write v8:


  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v8 -- v7 + carry-free rounding outputs.\n// Rounding increment uses precomputed prefix-ANDs of the pre-round fraction,\n// so round_up (late) reaches every fraction bit through a single XOR.\n// Normal and subnormal rounding datapaths run fully in parallel.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- unpack ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire ez_a = ~|ea, ez_b = ~|eb;\n  wire eo_a = &ea,  eo_b = &eb;\n  wire fz_a = ~|fa, fz_b = ~|fb;\n  wire zero_a = ez_a & fz_a,  zero_b = ez_b & fz_b;\n  wire inf_a  = eo_a & fz_a,  inf_b  = eo_b & fz_b;\n  wire nan_a  = eo_a & ~fz_a, nan_b  = eo_b & ~fz_b;\n  wire sub_a  = ez_a & ~fz_a, sub_b  = ez_b & ~fz_b;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count of the 10-bit fractions ----\n  wire [3:0] lz_a = fa[9] ? 4'd0 : fa[8] ? 4'd1 : fa[7] ? 4'd2 : fa[6] ? 4'd3 :\n                    fa[5] ? 4'd4 : fa[4] ? 4'd5 : fa[3] ? 4'd6 : fa[2] ? 4'd7 :\n                    fa[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n  wire [3:0] lz_b = fb[9] ? 4'd0 : fb[8] ? 4'd1 : fb[7] ? 4'd2 : fb[6] ? 4'd3 :\n                    fb[5] ? 4'd4 : fb[4] ? 4'd5 : fb[3] ? 4'd6 : fb[2] ? 4'd7 :\n                    fb[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n\n  // ---- normalized significands (leading 1 at bit 10) ----\n  wire [10:0] ma = sub_a ? (e0 >> 1) : e0;\n  wire        o1 = R[0] & e0[0];\n  wire [11:0] e2 = R[1] ? (e1 >> 2) : e1;\n  wire        o2 = R[1] & (|e1[1:0]);\n  wire [11:0] e4 = R[2] ? (e2 >> 4) : e2;\n  wire        o4 = R[2] & (|e2[3:0]);\n  wire [11:0] e8 = R[3] ? (e4 >> 8) : e4;\n  wire        o8 = R[3] & (|e4[7:0]);\n\n  wire [9:0] frac_s  = e8[10:1];\n  wire       guard_s = e8[0];\n  wire       sticky_s = (|p[9:0]) | o1 | o2 | o4 | o8;\n  wire       round_s = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_s[0]);\n\n  wire [9:0] pa_s;              // prefix-AND: pa_s[i] = &frac_s[i:0]\n  assign pa_s[0] = frac_s[0];\n  genvar i;\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1) assign pa_s[i] = pa_s[i-1] & frac_s[i];\n  endgenerate\n  wire [9:0] frac_s_o;\n  assign frac_s_o[0] = frac_s[0] ^ round_s;\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1)\n      assign frac_s_o[i] = frac_s[i] ^ (round_s & pa_s[i-1]);\n  endgenerate\n  wire carry_s = round_s & pa_s[9];\n\n  // ================= normal rounding path =================\n  wire [9:0] frac_na = p[19:10];      // if p_hi = 0\n  wire [9:0] frac_nb = p[20:11];      // if p_hi = 1\n  wire [9:0] frac_n  = p_hi ? frac_nb : frac_na;\n  wire       guard_n = p_hi ? p[10] : p[9];\n  wire       sticky_n = p_hi ? (|p[9:0]) : (|p[8:0]);\n  wire       round_n = guard_n & (sticky_n | frac_n[0]);\n\n  wire [9:0] pa_na, pa_nb;            // prefix-ANDs computed in parallel\n  assign pa_na[0] = frac_na[0];\n  assign pa_nb[0] = frac_nb[0];\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1) begin : gpa\n      assign pa_na[i] = pa_na[i-1] & frac_na[i];\n      assign pa_nb[i] = pa_nb[i-1] & frac_nb[i];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  wire [9:0] pa_n = p_hi ? pa_nb : pa_na;\n  wire [9:0] frac_n_o;\n  assign frac_n_o[0] = frac_n[0] ^ round_n;\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1)\n      assign frac_n_o[i] = frac_n[i] ^ (round_n & pa_n[i-1]);\n  endgenerate\n  wire carry_n = round_n & pa_n[9];\n\n  // ================= merge =================\n  wire [9:0] frac_o = is_sub ? frac_s_o : frac_n_o;\n  wire       carry  = is_sub ? carry_s  : carry_n;\n  wire       round_up = is_sub ? round_s : round_n;   // (unused further, kept for clarity)\n\n  // ---- final exponent ----\n  wire [1:0] ksel = {1'b0, p_hi} + {1'b0, carry_n};   // 0,1,2\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm = (ksel == 2'd0) ? em0 : (ksel == 2'd1) ? em1 : em2;\n  wire [4:0] exp_o    = is_sub ? {4'd0, carry_s} : exp_norm;\n  wire       ovf      = ovf_cmp | (t_eq45 & carry_n);\n\n  // ---- flattened packing ----\n  wire not_special = ~nan_in & ~inf_in & ~zero_in;\n  wire gate_f      = not_special & ~ovf;\n\n  wire [9:0] frac_y;\n  assign frac_y[8:0] = frac_o[8:0] & {9{gate_f}};\n  assign frac_y[9]   = nan_in | (frac_o[9] & gate_f);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y = (nan_in | inf_in) ? 5'd31 :\n                     zero_in ? 5'd0 :\n                     ovf ? 5'd31 : exp_o;\n\n  wire sign_y = sy & ~nan_in;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='v8.v')

[ASSISTANT]
v8 passes at **102784** (delay 1156, area 88.9). The critical path ends at `y[10]` (exponent LSB) going through the subnormal funnel shifter → prefix-AND carry chain → rounding. I suspect my manual prefix-AND (`pa[i]=pa[i-1]&frac[i]`) is a **linear ripple** (~10 levels), which is the bottleneck. Let me replace the manual prefix-AND rounding with a simple adder form and let Yosys build a balanced carry structure. Let me create v9:


  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v9 -- v8 but rounding via adder form (let Yosys build a fast\n// balanced incrementer) instead of a hand-rippled prefix-AND chain.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- unpack ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire ez_a = ~|ea, ez_b = ~|eb;\n  wire eo_a = &ea,  eo_b = &eb;\n  wire fz_a = ~|fa, fz_b = ~|fb;\n  wire zero_a = ez_a & fz_a,  zero_b = ez_b & fz_b;\n  wire inf_a  = eo_a & fz_a,  inf_b  = eo_b & fz_b;\n  wire nan_a  = eo_a & ~fz_a, nan_b  = eo_b & ~fz_b;\n  wire sub_a  = ez_a & ~fz_a, sub_b  = ez_b & ~fz_b;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count of the 10-bit fractions ----\n  wire [3:0] lz_a = fa[9] ? 4'd0 : fa[8] ? 4'd1 : fa[7] ? 4'd2 : fa[6] ? 4'd3 :\n                    fa[5] ? 4'd4 : fa[4] ? 4'd5 : fa[3] ? 4'd6 : fa[2] ? 4'd7 :\n                    fa[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n  wire [3:0] lz_b = fb[9] ? 4'd0 : fb[8] ? 4'd1 : fb[7] ? 4'd2 : fb[6] ? 4'd3 :\n                    fb[5] ? 4'd4 : fb[4] ? 4'd5 : fb[3] ? 4'd6 : fb[2] ? 4'd7 :\n                    fb[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n\n  // ---- normalized significands (leading 1 at bit 10) ----\n  wire [10:0] ma = sub_a ? ({fa, 1'b0} << lz_a) : {1'b1, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb = sub_b ? ({fb, 1'b0} << lz_b) : {1'b1, fb};\n\n  // ---- exponent sum (early) ----\n  wire signed [6:0] ea_e = sub_a ? -$signed({3'b000, lz_a}) : $signed({2'b00, ea});\n  wire signed [6:0] eb_e = sub_b ? -$signed({3'b000, lz_b}) : $signed({2'b00, eb});\n  wire signed [6:0] t_base = ea_e + eb_e;   // in [-18, 60]\n\n  wire tb_le14 = (t_base <= 7'sd14);\n  wire tb_le15 = (t_base <= 7'sd15);\n  wire tb_ge45 = (t_base >= 7'sd45);\n  wire tb_ge46 = (t_base >= 7'sd46);\n  wire tb_eq44 = (t_base == 7'sd44);\n  wire tb_eq45 = (t_base == 7'sd45);\n\n  wire [4:0] em0 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd15;\n  wire [4:0] em1 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd14;\n  wire [4:0] em2 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd13;\n\n  wire [3:0] R = (t_base >= 7'sd0) ? (4'd15 - t_base[3:0]) : 4'd15;\n\n  // ---- multiply ----\n  wire [21:0] p    = ma * mb;\n  wire        p_hi = p[21];\n\n  wire is_sub  = p_hi ? tb_le14 : tb_le15;\n  wire ovf_cmp = p_hi ? tb_ge45 : tb_ge46;\n  wire t_eq45  = p_hi ? tb_eq44 : tb_eq45;\n\n  // ================= subnormal rounding path =================\n  wire [11:0] e0 = p[21:10];\n  wire [11:0] e1 = R[0] ? (e0 >> 1) : e0;\n  wire        o1 = R[0] & e0[0];\n  wire [11:0] e2 = R[1] ? (e1 >> 2) : e1;\n  wire        o2 = R[1] & (|e1[1:0]);\n  wire [11:0] e4 = R[2] ? (e2 >> 4) : e2;\n  wire        o4 = R[2] & (|e2[3:0]);\n  wire [11:0] e8 = R[3] ? (e4 >> 8) : e4;\n  wire        o8 = R[3] & (|e4[7:0]);\n\n  wire [9:0] frac_s  = e8[10:1];\n  wire       guard_s = e8[0];\n  wire       sticky_s = (|p[9:0]) | o1 | o2 | o4 | o8;\n  wire       round_s = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_s[0]);\n\n  wire [10:0] fr_s   = {1'b0, frac_s} + {10'd0, round_s};\n  wire [9:0]  frac_s_o = fr_s[9:0];\n  wire        carry_s  = fr_s[10];\n\n  // ================= normal rounding path =================\n  wire [9:0] frac_na = p[19:10];\n  wire [9:0] frac_nb = p[20:11];\n  wire [9:0] frac_n  = p_hi ? frac_nb : frac_na;\n  wire       guard_n = p_hi ? p[10] : p[9];\n  wire       sticky_n = p_hi ? (|p[9:0]) : (|p[8:0]);\n  wire       round_n = guard_n & (sticky_n | frac_n[0]);\n\n  wire [10:0] fr_n   = {1'b0, frac_n} + {10'd0, round_n};\n  wire [9:0]  frac_n_o = fr_n[9:0];\n  wire        carry_n  = fr_n[10];\n\n  // ================= merge =================\n  wire [9:0] frac_o = is_sub ? frac_s_o : frac_n_o;\n  wire       carry  = is_sub ? carry_s  : carry_n;\n\n  // ---- final exponent ----\n  wire [1:0] ksel = {1'b0, p_hi} + {1'b0, carry_n};\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm = (ksel == 2'd0) ? em0 : (ksel == 2'd1) ? em1 : em2;\n  wire [4:0] exp_o    = is_sub ? {4'd0, carry_s} : exp_norm;\n  wire       ovf      = ovf_cmp | (t_eq45 & carry_n);\n\n  // ---- flattened packing ----\n  wire not_special = ~nan_in & ~inf_in & ~zero_in;\n  wire gate_f      = not_special & ~ovf;\n\n  wire [9:0] frac_y;\n  assign frac_y[8:0] = frac_o[8:0] & {9{gate_f}};\n  assign frac_y[9]   = nan_in | (frac_o[9] & gate_f);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y = (nan_in | inf_in) ? 5'd31 :\n                     zero_in ? 5'd0 :\n                     ovf ? 5'd31 : exp_o;\n\n  wire sign_y = sy & ~nan_in;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='v9.v')

[ASSISTANT]
v9 (107375) is worse than v8 (102784) — the hand-written prefix-AND keeps fraction bits fast (single XOR after late `round`). Let me replace the ripple prefix-AND with a balanced **Kogge-Stone parallel-prefix AND** so every prefix (and carry) is ~4 levels, for both rounding paths. Creating v10:


  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v10 -- v8 with balanced (Kogge-Stone) parallel-prefix AND for\n// the rounding increment, replacing the linear ripple prefix chain. Every\n// prefix pa[i]=&frac[i:0] (and carry) is ~log2(10) levels, computed in\n// parallel with the late round signal.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- parallel-prefix AND: pfx[i] = &x[i:0] ----\n  // (Kogge-Stone; 10-bit, 4 stages)\n  function automatic [9:0] pfx_and10;\n    input [9:0] x;\n    reg [9:0] g;\n    integer i;\n    begin\n      g = x;                                   // stage d=0\n      // d=1\n      for (i=9;i>=1;i=i-1) g[i] = g[i] & g[i-1];\n      // d=2\n      for (i=9;i>=2;i=i-1) g[i] = g[i] & g[i-2];\n      // d=4\n      for (i=9;i>=4;i=i-1) g[i] = g[i] & g[i-4];\n      // d=8\n      for (i=9;i>=8;i=i-1) g[i] = g[i] & g[i-8];\n      pfx_and10 = g;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // ---- unpack ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire ez_a = ~|ea, ez_b = ~|eb;\n  wire eo_a = &ea,  eo_b = &eb;\n  wire fz_a = ~|fa, fz_b = ~|fb;\n  wire zero_a = ez_a & fz_a,  zero_b = ez_b & fz_b;\n  wire inf_a  = eo_a & fz_a,  inf_b  = eo_b & fz_b;\n  wire nan_a  = eo_a & ~fz_a, nan_b  = eo_b & ~fz_b;\n  wire sub_a  = ez_a & ~fz_a, sub_b  = ez_b & ~fz_b;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count of the 10-bit fractions ----\n  wire [3:0] lz_a = fa[9] ? 4'd0 : fa[8] ? 4'd1 : fa[7] ? 4'd2 : fa[6] ? 4'd3 :\n                    fa[5] ? 4'd4 : fa[4] ? 4'd5 : fa[3] ? 4'd6 : fa[2] ? 4'd7 :\n                    fa[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n  wire [3:0] lz_b = fb[9] ? 4'd0 : fb[8] ? 4'd1 : fb[7] ? 4'd2 : fb[6] ? 4'd3 :\n                    fb[5] ? 4'd4 : fb[4] ? 4'd5 : fb[3] ? 4'd6 : fb[2] ? 4'd7 :\n                    fb[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n\n  // ---- normalized significands (leading 1 at bit 10) ----\n  wire [10:0] ma = sub_a ? ({fa, 1'b0} << lz_a) : {1'b1, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb = sub_b ? ({fb, 1'b0} << lz_b) : {1'b1, fb};\n\n  // ---- exponent sum (early) ----\n  wire signed [6:0] ea_e = sub_a ? -$signed({3'b000, lz_a}) : $signed({2'b00, ea});\n  wire signed [6:0] eb_e = sub_b ? -$signed({3'b000, lz_b}) : $signed({2'b00, eb});\n  wire signed [6:0] t_base = ea_e + eb_e;\n\n  wire tb_le14 = (t_base <= 7'sd14);\n  wire tb_le15 = (t_base <= 7'sd15);\n  wire tb_ge45 = (t_base >= 7'sd45);\n  wire tb_ge46 = (t_base >= 7'sd46);\n  wire tb_eq44 = (t_base == 7'sd44);\n  wire tb_eq45 = (t_base == 7'sd45);\n\n  wire [4:0] em0 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd15;\n  wire [4:0] em1 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd14;\n  wire [4:0] em2 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd13;\n\n  wire [3:0] R = (t_base >= 7'sd0) ? (4'd15 - t_base[3:0]) : 4'd15;\n\n  // ---- multiply ----\n  wire [21:0] p    = ma * mb;\n  wire        p_hi = p[21];\n\n  wire is_sub  = p_hi ? tb_le14 : tb_le15;\n  wire ovf_cmp = p_hi ? tb_ge45 : tb_ge46;\n  wire t_eq45  = p_hi ? tb_eq44 : tb_eq45;\n\n  // ================= subnormal rounding path =================\n  wire [11:0] e0 = p[21:10];\n  wire [11:0] e1 = R[0] ? (e0 >> 1) : e0;\n  wire        o1 = R[0] & e0[0];\n  wire [11:0] e2 = R[1] ? (e1 >> 2) : e1;\n  wire        o2 = R[1] & (|e1[1:0]);\n  wire [11:0] e4 = R[2] ? (e2 >> 4) : e2;\n  wire        o4 = R[2] & (|e2[3:0]);\n  wire [11:0] e8 = R[3] ? (e4 >> 8) : e4;\n  wire        o8 = R[3] & (|e4[7:0]);\n\n  wire [9:0] frac_s  = e8[10:1];\n  wire       guard_s = e8[0];\n  wire       sticky_s = (|p[9:0]) | o1 | o2 | o4 | o8;\n  wire       round_s = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_s[0]);\n\n  wire [9:0] pa_s = pfx_and10(frac_s);\n  wire [9:0] frac_s_o;\n  assign frac_s_o[0] = frac_s[0] ^ round_s;\n  genvar i;\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1)\n      assign frac_s_o[i] = frac_s[i] ^ (round_s & pa_s[i-1]);\n  endgenerate\n  wire carry_s = round_s & pa_s[9];\n\n  // ================= normal rounding path =================\n  wire [9:0] frac_na = p[19:10];\n  wire [9:0] frac_nb = p[20:11];\n  wire [9:0] frac_n  = p_hi ? frac_nb : frac_na;\n  wire       guard_n = p_hi ? p[10] : p[9];\n  wire       sticky_n = p_hi ? (|p[9:0]) : (|p[8:0]);\n  wire       round_n = guard_n & (sticky_n | frac_n[0]);\n\n  wire [9:0] pa_na = pfx_and10(frac_na);\n  wire [9:0] pa_nb = pfx_and10(frac_nb);\n  wire [9:0] pa_n  = p_hi ? pa_nb : pa_na;\n  wire [9:0] frac_n_o;\n  assign frac_n_o[0] = frac_n[0] ^ round_n;\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1)\n      assign frac_n_o[i] = frac_n[i] ^ (round_n & pa_n[i-1]);\n  endgenerate\n  wire carry_n = round_n & pa_n[9];\n\n  // ================= merge =================\n  wire [9:0] frac_o = is_sub ? frac_s_o : frac_n_o;\n  wire       carry  = is_sub ? carry_s  : carry_n;\n\n  // ---- final exponent ----\n  wire [1:0] ksel = {1'b0, p_hi} + {1'b0, carry_n};\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm = (ksel == 2'd0) ? em0 : (ksel == 2'd1) ? em1 : em2;\n  wire [4:0] exp_o    = is_sub ? {4'd0, carry_s} : exp_norm;\n  wire       ovf      = ovf_cmp | (t_eq45 & carry_n);\n\n  // ---- flattened packing ----\n  wire not_special = ~nan_in & ~inf_in & ~zero_in;\n  wire gate_f      = not_special & ~ovf;\n\n  wire [9:0] frac_y;\n  assign frac_y[8:0] = frac_o[8:0] & {9{gate_f}};\n  assign frac_y[9]   = nan_in | (frac_o[9] & gate_f);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y = (nan_in | inf_in) ? 5'd31 :\n                     zero_in ? 5'd0 :\n                     ovf ? 5'd31 : exp_o;\n\n  wire sign_y = sy & ~nan_in;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='v10.v')

  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v11 -- one-sided folded subnormal multiply.\n// Subnormal a is folded into the multiplier's partial products (each product\n// row is shifted by lz_a via a 10:1 mux) instead of normalizing it before the\n// multiply.  The effective exponent uses 10*lz_a (== lz_a mod 32 in binary)\n// so lz_a enters the exponent path only as one-hot gated sums.\n// t_base = eb + 1 + (sub_a ? 10*lz_a - ea : ea)  -- fully pre-multiplier.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- unpack ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire ez_a = ~|ea, ez_b = ~|eb;\n  wire eo_a = &ea,  eo_b = &eb;\n  wire fz_a = ~|fa, fz_b = ~|fb;\n  wire zero_a = ez_a & fz_a,  zero_b = ez_b & fz_b;\n  wire inf_a  = eo_a & fz_a,  inf_b  = eo_b & fz_b;\n  wire nan_a  = eo_a & ~fz_a, nan_b  = eo_b & ~fz_b;\n  wire sub_a  = ez_a & ~fz_a, sub_b  = ez_b & ~fz_b;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- one-hot LZC of fa: lzh[i] = (lzc(fa) == i) ----\n  wire [9:0] lzh;\n  genvar j;\n  generate\n    for (j = 9; j >= 0; j = j - 1)\n      assign lzh[j] = fa[j] & (j == 9 ? 1'b1 : ~|fa[9:j+1]);\n  endgenerate\n  wire [3:0] lz_code = {lzh[9] | lzh[8] | lzh[7] | lzh[6] | lzh[5],\n                        lzh[9] | lzh[8] | lzh[7] | lzh[3] | lzh[2] | lzh[1],\n                        lzh[9] | lzh[7] | lzh[6] | lzh[5] | lzh[3] | lzh[1],\n                        lzh[9] | lzh[6] | lzh[2] | lzh[1]};\n\n  // ---- effective exponent of a: ea, or (10*lz - ea) when subnormal ----\n  wire [4:0] ten_lz = lzh[0] ? 5'd0 :  lzh[1] ? 5'd10 : lzh[2] ? 5'd20 :\n                      lzh[3] ? 5'd30 : lzh[4] ? 5'd8  : lzh[5] ? 5'd18 :\n                      lzh[6] ? 5'd28 : lzh[7] ? 5'd6  : lzh[8] ? 5'd16 : 5'd26;\n  wire [5:0] ea_eff = sub_a ? ({1'b0, ten_lz} - {1'b0, ea}) : {1'b0, ea};\n\n  // ---- t_base = ea_eff + eb + 1 (early, before the multiplier) ----\n  wire [6:0] t_base = {1'b0, ea_eff} + {2'b00, eb} + 7'd1;\n\n  wire tb_le15 = ~t_base[6] & (t_base[5:0] <= 6'd15);\n  wire tb_ge46 =  t_base[6] | (t_base[5:0] >= 6'd46);\n\n  // early exponent table: t_base - 15 + k, k = 0..1\n  wire [4:0] em0 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd15;\n  wire [4:0] em1 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd14;\n\n  // early subnormal shift amount R = clamp(15 - t_base, 0..15)\n  wire [4:0] R = tb_le15 ? (5'd15 - t_base[4:0]) : 5'd0;\n\n  // ---- shifted-rows multiplier:  sum of (fa << lz_a) * fb_bit[j] ----\n  wire [21:0] pp [0:10];\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k <= 10; k = k + 1) begin : gpp\n      wire [21:0] row;\n      wire [9:0]  fbk = fb | {10{fb[k]}};   // (unused placeholder removed)\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [21:0] prod;\n  genvar r;\n  generate\n    for (r = 0; r <= 10; r = r + 1) begin : grow\n      wire [21:0] shifted = (fa << lzh[0])  << (r ? 0 : 0); // replaced below\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  // explicit folded multiply (yosys will optimize the mux trees):\n  wire [9:0]  fa_n = sub_a ? ((fa << lzh[0]) | ((fa << 1) & {10{lzh[1]}}) |\n                              ((fa << 2) & {10{lzh[2]}}) | ((fa << 3) & {10{lzh[3]}}) |\n                              ((fa << 4) & {10{lzh[4]}}) | ((fa << 5) & {10{lzh[5]}}) |\n                              ((fa << 6) & {10{lzh[6]}}) | ((fa << 7) & {10{lzh[7]}}) |\n                              ((fa << 8) & {10{lzh[8]}}) | ((fa << 9) & {10{lzh[9]}}))\n                           : fa;\n  // Note: folded version = same as pre-normalize on side a only; kept for\n  // structural experimentation.  Multiply (fa_n . 11 bits hidden) * mb.\n  wire [10:0] ma = sub_a ? {1'b0, fa_n} : {1'b1, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb = sub_b ? ({fb, 1'b0} << lz_b_code) : {1'b1, fb};\n\n  wire [3:0] lz_b_code = fb[9] ? 4'd0 : fb[8] ? 4'd1 : fb[7] ? 4'd2 : fb[6] ? 4'd3 :\n                         fb[5] ? 4'd4 : fb[4] ? 4'd5 : fb[3] ? 4'd6 : fb[2] ? 4'd7 :\n                         fb[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n\n  wire [21:0] p = ma * mb;\n\n  // product p = (fa_n<<lz?) ... with side-a pre-normalized the leading one of\n  // the full product is at bit 20 or 21 exactly as in v4; but we only fold to\n  // test yosys freedom.  Use v4-style fields:\n  wire [11:0] mant = p[21:10];        // leading 1 at bit 10 of mant (bit20/21 of p)\n\n  // ================= subnormal rounding path =================\n  wire [11:0] e1 = R[0] ? (mant >> 1) : mant;\n  wire        o1 = R[0] & mant[0];\n  wire [11:0] e2 = R[1] ? (e1 >> 2) : e1;\n  wire        o2 = R[1] & (|e1[1:0]);\n  wire [11:0] e4 = R[2] ? (e4 >> 8) : e4;\n  wire        o8 = R[3] & (|e4[7:0]);\n\n  wire [9:0] frac_s  = e8[10:1];\n  wire       guard_s = e8[0];\n  wire       sticky_s = (|p[9:0]) | o1 | o2 | o4 | o8;\n  wire       round_s = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_s[0]);\n\n  wire [10:0] fr_s     = {1'b0, frac_s} + {10'd0, round_s};\n  wire [9:0]  frac_s_o = fr_s[9:0];\n  wire        carry_s  = fr_s[10];\n\n  // ================= normal rounding path =================\n  wire [9:0] frac_n  = mant[9:0];\n  wire       guard_n = p[9];\n  wire       sticky_n = |p[8:0];\n  wire       round_n = guard_n & (sticky_n | frac_n[0]);\n\n  wire [10:0] fr_n     = {1'b0, frac_n} + {10'd0, round_n};\n  wire [9:0]  frac_n_o = fr_n[9:0];\n  wire        carry_n  = fr_n[10];\n\n  // ================= merge =================\n  wire [9:0] frac_o = tb_le15 ? frac_s_o : frac_n_o;\n\n  // ---- final exponent ----\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm = carry_n ? em1 : em0;\n  wire [4:0] exp_o    = tb_le15 ? {4'd0, carry_s} : exp_norm;\n  wire       ovf      = tb_ge46;\n\n  // ---- flattened packing ----\n  wire not_special = ~nan_in & ~inf_in & ~zero_in;\n  wire gate_f      = not_special & ~ovf;\n\n  wire [9:0] frac_y;\n  assign frac_y[8:0] = frac_o[8:0] & {9{gate_f}};\n  assign frac_y[9]   = nan_in | (frac_o[9] & gate_f);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y = (nan_in | inf_in) ? 5'd31 :\n                     zero_in ? 5'd0 :\n                     ovf ? 5'd31 : exp_o;\n\n  wire sign_y = sy & ~nan_in;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='v11.v')

  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v12 -- v8 + flattened exponent packing.\n// Exponent output is bit-gated (OR/AND plane) instead of a 4-level mux chain,\n// mirroring the fraction gating already in v8.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- unpack ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire ez_a = ~|ea, ez_b = ~|eb;\n  wire eo_a = &ea,  eo_b = &eb;\n  wire fz_a = ~|fa, fz_b = ~|fb;\n  wire zero_a = ez_a & fz_a,  zero_b = ez_b & fz_b;\n  wire inf_a  = eo_a & fz_a,  inf_b  = eo_b & fz_b;\n  wire nan_a  = eo_a & ~fz_a, nan_b  = eo_b & ~fz_b;\n  wire sub_a  = ez_a & ~fz_a, sub_b  = ez_b & ~fz_b;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count of the 10-bit fractions ----\n  wire [3:0] lz_a = fa[9] ? 4'd0 : fa[8] ? 4'd1 : fa[7] ? 4'd2 : fa[6] ? 4'd3 :\n                    fa[5] ? 4'd4 : fa[4] ? 4'd5 : fa[3] ? 4'd6 : fa[2] ? 4'd7 :\n                    fa[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n  wire [3:0] lz_b = fb[9] ? 4'd0 : fb[8] ? 4'd1 : fb[7] ? 4'd2 : fb[6] ? 4'd3 :\n                    fb[5] ? 4'd4 : fb[4] ? 4'd5 : fb[3] ? 4'd6 : fb[2] ? 4'd7 :\n                    fb[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n\n  // ---- normalized significands (leading 1 at bit 10) ----\n  wire [10:0] ma = sub_a ? ({fa, 1'b0} << lz_a) : {1'b1, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb = sub_b ? ({fb, 1'b0} << lz_b) : {1'b1, fb};\n\n  // ---- exponent sum (early) ----\n  wire signed [6:0] ea_e = sub_a ? -$signed({3'b000, lz_a}) : $signed({2'b00, ea});\n  wire signed [6:0] eb_e = sub_b ? -$signed({3'b000, lz_b}) : $signed({2'b00, eb});\n  wire signed [6:0] t_base = ea_e + eb_e;\n\n  wire tb_le14 = (t_base <= 7'sd14);\n  wire tb_le15 = (t_base <= 7'sd15);\n  wire tb_ge45 = (t_base >= 7'sd45);\n  wire tb_ge46 = (t_base >= 7'sd46);\n  wire tb_eq44 = (t_base == 7'sd44);\n  wire tb_eq45 = (t_base == 7'sd45);\n\n  wire [4:0] em0 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd15;\n  wire [4:0] em1 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd14;\n  wire [4:0] em2 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd13;\n\n  wire [3:0] R = (t_base >= 7'sd0) ? (4'd15 - t_base[3:0]) : 4'd15;\n\n  // ---- multiply ----\n  wire [21:0] p    = ma * mb;\n  wire        p_hi = p[21];\n\n  wire is_sub  = p_hi ? tb_le14 : tb_le15;\n  wire ovf_cmp = p_hi ? tb_ge45 : tb_ge46;\n  wire t_eq45  = p_hi ? tb_eq44 : tb_eq45;\n\n  // ================= subnormal rounding path =================\n  wire [11:0] e0 = p[21:10];\n  wire [11:0] e1 = R[0] ? (e0 >> 1) : e0;\n  wire        o1 = R[0] & e0[0];\n  wire [11:0] e2 = R[1] ? (e1 >> 2) : e1;\n  wire        o2 = R[1] & (|e1[1:0]);\n  wire [11:0] e4 = R[2] ? (e2 >> 4) : e2;\n  wire        o4 = R[2] & (|e2[3:0]);\n  wire [11:0] e8 = R[3] ? (e4 >> 8) : e4;\n  wire        o8 = R[3] & (|e4[7:0]);\n\n  wire [9:0] frac_s  = e8[10:1];\n  wire       guard_s = e8[0];\n  wire       sticky_s = (|p[9:0]) | o1 | o2 | o4 | o8;\n  wire       round_s = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_s[0]);\n\n  wire [9:0] pa_s;\n  assign pa_s[0] = frac_s[0];\n  genvar i;\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1) assign pa_s[i] = pa_s[i-1] & frac_s[i];\n  endgenerate\n  wire [9:0] frac_s_o;\n  assign frac_s_o[0] = frac_s[0] ^ round_s;\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1)\n      assign frac_s_o[i] = frac_s[i] ^ (round_s & pa_s[i-1]);\n  endgenerate\n  wire carry_s = round_s & pa_s[9];\n\n  // ================= normal rounding path =================\n  wire [9:0] frac_na = p[19:10];\n  wire [9:0] frac_nb = p[20:11];\n  wire [9:0] frac_n  = p_hi ? frac_nb : frac_na;\n  wire       guard_n = p_hi ? p[10] : p[9];\n  wire       sticky_n = p_hi ? (|p[9:0]) : (|p[8:0]);\n  wire       round_n = guard_n & (sticky_n | frac_n[0]);\n\n  wire [9:0] pa_na, pa_nb;\n  assign pa_na[0] = frac_na[0];\n  assign pa_nb[0] = frac_nb[0];\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1) begin : gpa\n      assign pa_na[i] = pa_na[i-1] & frac_na[i];\n      assign pa_nb[i] = pa_nb[i-1] & frac_nb[i];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  wire [9:0] pa_n = p_hi ? pa_nb : pa_na;\n  wire [9:0] frac_n_o;\n  assign frac_n_o[0] = frac_n[0] ^ round_n;\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1)\n      assign frac_n_o[i] = frac_n[i] ^ (round_n & pa_n[i-1]);\n  endgenerate\n  wire carry_n = round_n & pa_n[9];\n\n  // ================= merge =================\n  wire [9:0] frac_o = is_sub ? frac_s_o : frac_n_o;\n  wire       carry  = is_sub ? carry_s  : carry_n;\n\n  // ---- final exponent (flat) ----\n  wire [1:0] ksel = {1'b0, p_hi} + {1'b0, carry_n};\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm = (ksel == 2'd0) ? em0 : (ksel == 2'd1) ? em1 : em2;\n  wire [4:0] exp_o    = is_sub ? {4'd0, carry_s} : exp_norm;\n  wire       ovf      = ovf_cmp | (t_eq45 & carry_n);\n\n  // exp_y = 31 when nan|inf|ovf ; 0 when zero_in ; else exp_o\n  wire       nif     = nan_in | inf_in;\n  wire       force31 = nif | ovf;\n  wire [4:0] exp_y;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 5; k = k + 1)\n      assign exp_y[k] = force31 | (exp_o[k] & ~zero_in & ~force31);\n  endgenerate\n\n  // ---- flattened packing ----\n  wire not_special = ~nan_in & ~inf_in & ~zero_in;\n  wire gate_f      = not_special & ~ovf;\n\n  wire [9:0] frac_y;\n  assign frac_y[8:0] = frac_o[8:0] & {9{gate_f}};\n  assign frac_y[9]   = nan_in | (frac_o[9] & gate_f);\n\n  wire sign_y = sy & ~nan_in;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='v12.v')

[ASSISTANT]
v12 is worse (109257) — Yosys's mux tree was already good; sticking with v8 structure. Next bigger idea: **completely eliminate the funnel shifter and frac/guard/sticky muxes** — use early one-hot decoding of the shift amount `R`, and with mode-embedded selects (`is_sub`, `p_hi`), express every output fraction bit, guard, and sticky as a single AND-OR plane directly from the multiplier bits `p`. This replaces 4 mux levels + 1 mux with roughly 3 gate levels. Creating v13:


  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v13 -- v8 core, but the subnormal alignment + normal/subnormal\n// frac/guard/sticky selection is a single AND-OR plane off the multiplier\n// product bits (one-hot shift decode, mode selects embedded), replacing the\n// funnel shifter and its muxes.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- unpack ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire ez_a = ~|ea, ez_b = ~|eb;\n  wire eo_a = &ea,  eo_b = &eb;\n  wire fz_a = ~|fa, fz_b = ~|fb;\n  wire zero_a = ez_a & fz_a,  zero_b = ez_b & fz_b;\n  wire inf_a  = eo_a & fz_a,  inf_b  = eo_b & fz_b;\n  wire nan_a  = eo_a & ~fz_a, nan_b  = eo_b & ~fz_b;\n  wire sub_a  = ez_a & ~fz_a, sub_b  = ez_b & ~fz_b;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count of the 10-bit fractions ----\n  wire [3:0] lz_a = fa[9] ? 4'd0 : fa[8] ? 4'd1 : fa[7] ? 4'd2 : fa[6] ? 4'd3 :\n                    fa[5] ? 4'd4 : fa[4] ? 4'd5 : fa[3] ? 4'd6 : fa[2] ? 4'd7 :\n                    fa[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n  wire [3:0] lz_b = fb[9] ? 4'd0 : fb[8] ? 4'd1 : fb[7] ? 4'd2 : fb[6] ? 4'd3 :\n                    fb[5] ? 4'd4 : fb[4] ? 4'd5 : fb[3] ? 4'd6 : fb[2] ? 4'd7 :\n                    fb[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n\n  // ---- normalized significands (leading 1 at bit 10) ----\n  wire [10:0] ma = sub_a ? ({fa, 1'b0} << lz_a) : {1'b1, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb = sub_b ? ({fb, 1'b0} << lz_b) : {1'b1, fb};\n\n  // ---- exponent sum (early) ----\n  wire signed [6:0] ea_e = sub_a ? -$signed({3'b000, lz_a}) : $signed({2'b00, ea});\n  wire signed [6:0] eb_e = sub_b ? -$signed({3'b000, lz_b}) : $signed({2'b00, eb});\n  wire signed [6:0] t_base = ea_e + eb_e;\n\n  wire tb_le14 = (t_base <= 7'sd14);\n  wire tb_le15 = (t_base <= 7'sd15);\n  wire tb_ge45 = (t_base >= 7'sd45);\n  wire tb_ge46 = (t_base >= 7'sd46);\n  wire tb_eq44 = (t_base == 7'sd44);\n  wire tb_eq45 = (t_base == 7'sd45);\n\n  wire [4:0] em0 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd15;\n  wire [4:0] em1 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd14;\n  wire [4:0] em2 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd13;\n\n  wire [3:0] R = (t_base >= 7'sd0) ? (4'd15 - t_base[3:0]) : 4'd15;\n\n  // ---- one-hot decode of R ----\n  wire [15:0] Req;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 16; k = k + 1)\n      assign Req[k] = (R == k[3:0]);\n  endgenerate\n\n  // ---- multiply ----\n  wire [21:0] p    = ma * mb;\n  wire        p_hi = p[21];\n\n  wire is_sub  = p_hi ? tb_le14 : tb_le15;\n  wire ovf_cmp = p_hi ? tb_ge45 : tb_ge46;\n  wire t_eq45  = p_hi ? tb_eq44 : tb_eq45;\n\n  // ---- mode selects (late: depend on p_hi) ----\n  wire is_nor = ~is_sub;\n  wire sel_n0 = is_nor & ~p_hi;    // normal, product leading 1 at bit 20\n  wire sel_n1 = is_nor &  p_hi;    // normal, product leading 1 at bit 21\n  wire [15:0] sel_s;               // subnormal, shift by R\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 16; k = k + 1)\n      assign sel_s[k] = is_sub & Req[k];\n  endgenerate\n\n  // ---- AND-OR plane: frac_o[i] = p[11+k+i] selected by sel_s[k],\n  //      or p[10+i] / p[11+i] for normal ----\n  wire [9:0] frac_o;\n  genvar i;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 10; i = i + 1) begin : gfrac\n      wire [12:0] term;\n      genvar kk;\n      for (kk = 0; kk < 11; kk = kk + 1) begin : gs\n        if (11 + kk + i <= 21)\n          assign term[kk] = sel_s[kk] & p[11+kk+i];\n        else\n          assign term[kk] = 1'b0;\n      end\n      assign term[11] = sel_n0 & p[10+i];\n      assign term[12] = sel_n1 & p[11+i];\n      assign frac_o[i] = |term;\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  // ---- guard ----\n  wire [12:0] gterm;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 12; k = k + 1)\n      assign gterm[k] = sel_s[k] & p[10+k];\n  endgenerate\n  assign gterm[12] = sel_n0 & p[9];\n  wire guard = (|gterm[12:12]) | sel_n1 & p[10] | (|gterm[11:0]);\n\n  // ---- sticky: prefix OR of p gives st[k] = |p[9+k:0] ----\n  wire [21:0] pfx;\n  assign pfx[0] = p[0];\n  genvar q;\n  generate\n    for (q = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1) assign pa[i] = pa[i-1] & frac_o[i];\n  endgenerate\n  wire [9:0] frac_r;\n  assign frac_r[0] = frac_o[0] ^ round_up;\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1)\n      assign frac_r[i] = frac_o[i] ^ (round_up & pa[i-1]);\n  endgenerate\n  wire carry = round_up & pa[9];\n\n  // ---- final exponent ----\n  wire [1:0] ksel = {1'b0, p_hi} + {1'b0, carry};\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm = (ksel == 2'd0) ? em0 : (ksel == 2'd1) ? em1 : em2;\n  wire [4:0] exp_o    = is_sub ? {4'd0, carry} : exp_norm;\n  wire       ovf      = ovf_cmp | (t_eq45 & carry);\n\n  // ---- flattened packing ----\n  wire not_special = ~nan_in & ~inf_in & ~zero_in;\n  wire gate_f      = not_special & ~ovf;\n\n  wire [9:0] frac_y;\n  assign frac_y[8:0] = frac_r[8:0] & {9{gate_f}};\n  assign frac_y[9]   = nan_in | (frac_r[9] & gate_f);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y = (nan_in | inf_in) ? 5'd31 :\n                     zero_in ? 5'd0 :\n                     ovf ? 5'd31 : exp_o;\n\n  wire sign_y = sy & ~nan_in;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='v13.v')
