[ASSISTANT]
The critical path analysis shows the post-multiplier alignment (funnel shifter + sticky + rounding) is the largest section (~420ps). The subnormal shift amount `R` is known **early** (from `t_base`, before the multiplier), so I can replace the 4-stage funnel shifter with an early one-hot-decoded AND-OR plane. Let me create v14 (v8 with only the subnormal path restructured, normal path untouched):


  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v14 -- v8 core; the subnormal alignment (funnel shifter) is\n// replaced by an early one-hot-decoded AND-OR plane.  R = 15 - t_base is known\n// before the multiplier, so each subnormal frac/guard/sticky bit is a single\n// AND-OR level off the product bits, removing the 4-stage funnel mux chain.\n// The normal path is left as direct bit-selects (unchanged from v8).\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- unpack ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire ez_a = ~|ea, ez_b = ~|eb;\n  wire eo_a = &ea,  eo_b = &eb;\n  wire fz_a = ~|fa, fz_b = ~|fb;\n  wire zero_a = ez_a & fz_a,  zero_b = ez_b & fz_b;\n  wire inf_a  = eo_a & fz_a,  inf_b  = eo_b & fz_b;\n  wire nan_a  = eo_a & ~fz_a, nan_b  = eo_b & ~fz_b;\n  wire sub_a  = ez_a & ~fz_a, sub_b  = ez_b & ~fz_b;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count of the 10-bit fractions ----\n  wire [3:0] lz_a = fa[9] ? 4'd0 : fa[8] ? 4'd1 : fa[7] ? 4'd2 : fa[6] ? 4'd3 :\n                    fa[5] ? 4'd4 : fa[4] ? 4'd5 : fa[3] ? 4'd6 : fa[2] ? 4'd7 :\n                    fa[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n  wire [3:0] lz_b = fb[9] ? 4'd0 : fb[8] ? 4'd1 : fb[7] ? 4'd2 : fb[6] ? 4'd3 :\n                    fb[5] ? 4'd4 : fb[4] ? 4'd5 : fb[3] ? 4'd6 : fb[2] ? 4'd7 :\n                    fb[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n\n  // ---- normalized significands (leading 1 at bit 10) ----\n  wire [10:0] ma = sub_a ? ({fa, 1'b0} << lz_a) : {1'b1, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb = sub_b ? ({fb, 1'b0} << lz_b) : {1'b1, fb};\n\n  // ---- exponent sum (early) ----\n  wire signed [6:0] ea_e = sub_a ? -$signed({3'b000, lz_a}) : $signed({2'b00, ea});\n  wire signed [6:0] eb_e = sub_b ? -$signed({3'b000, lz_b}) : $signed({2'b00, eb});\n  wire signed [6:0] t_base = ea_e + eb_e;\n\n  wire tb_le14 = (t_base <= 7'sd14);\n  wire tb_le15 = (t_base <= 7'sd15);\n  wire tb_ge45 = (t_base >= 7'sd45);\n  wire tb_ge46 = (t_base >= 7'sd46);\n  wire tb_eq44 = (t_base == 7'sd44);\n  wire tb_eq45 = (t_base == 7'sd45);\n\n  wire [4:0] em0 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd15;\n  wire [4:0] em1 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd14;\n  wire [4:0] em2 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd13;\n\n  wire [3:0] R = (t_base >= 7'sd0) ? (4'd15 - t_base[3:0]) : 4'd15;\n\n  // ---- one-hot decode of R (early) ----\n  wire [15:0] Req;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 16; k = k + 1)\n      assign Req[k] = (R == k[3:0]);\n  endgenerate\n\n  // ---- multiply ----\n  wire [21:0] p    = ma * mb;\n  wire        p_hi = p[21];\n\n  wire is_sub  = p_hi ? tb_le14 : tb_le15;\n  wire ovf_cmp = p_hi ? tb_ge45 : tb_ge46;\n  wire t_eq45  = p_hi ? tb_eq44 : tb_eq45;\n\n  // ---- prefix OR of p for sticky (balanced, in parallel with the AND-OR) ----\n  wire [21:0] pfx;\n  assign pfx[0] = p[0];\n  genvar q;\n  generate\n    for (q = 1; q < 22; q = q + 1) assign pfx[q] = pfx[q-1] | p[q];\n  endgenerate\n\n  // ================= subnormal path: AND-OR plane =================\n  // frac_s[i] = OR_k Req[k] & p[10+k+i]  (k = 0..11, valid when 10+k+i <= 21)\n  wire [9:0] frac_s;\n  genvar i;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 10; i = i + 1) begin : gfs\n      wire [11:0] t;\n      genvar kk;\n      for (kk = 0; kk < 12; kk = kk + 1) begin : gt\n        if (10 + kk + i <= 21)\n          assign t[kk] = Req[kk] & p[10+kk+i];\n        else\n          assign t[kk] = 1'b0;\n      end\n      assign frac_s[i] = |t;\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  // guard_s = OR_k Req[k] & p[10+k], k=0..11\n  wire [11:0] gt;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 12; k = k + 1)\n      assign gt[k] = Req[k] & p[10+k];\n  endgenerate\n  wire guard_s = |gt;\n\n  // sticky_s = OR_k Req[k] & pfx[9+k] (clamped to pfx[21])\n  wire [15:0] st;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 16; k = k + 1) begin : gst\n      assign st[k] = Req[k] & ((9+k <= 21) ? pfx[9+k] : pfx[21]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  wire sticky_s = |st;\n\n  wire round_s = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_s[0]);\n\n  wire [9:0] pa_s;\n  assign pa_s[0] = frac_s[0];\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1) assign pa_s[i] = pa_s[i-1] & frac_s[i];\n  endgenerate\n  wire [9:0] frac_s_o;\n  assign frac_s_o[0] = frac_s[0] ^ round_s;\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1)\n      assign frac_s_o[i] = frac_s[i] ^ (round_s & pa_s[i-1]);\n  endgenerate\n  wire carry_s = round_s & pa_s[9];\n\n  // ================= normal rounding path (unchanged from v8) =================\n  wire [20:0] mant = p_hi ? p[21:1] : p[20:0];\n  wire [9:0] frac_n  = mant[19:10];\n  wire       guard_n = mant[9];\n  wire       sticky_n = (|mant[8:0]) | (p_hi & p[0]);\n  wire       round_n = guard_n & (sticky_n | frac_n[0]);\n\n  wire [9:0] pa_n;\n  assign pa_n[0] = frac_n[0];\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1) assign pa_n[i] = pa_n[i-1] & frac_n[i];\n  endgenerate\n  wire [9:0] frac_n_o;\n  assign frac_n_o[0] = frac_n[0] ^ round_n;\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1)\n      assign frac_n_o[i] = frac_n[i] ^ (round_n & pa_n[i-1]);\n  endgenerate\n  wire carry_n = round_n & pa_n[9];\n\n  // ================= merge =================\n  wire [9:0] frac_o = is_sub ? frac_s_o : frac_n_o;\n\n  // ---- final exponent ----\n  wire [1:0] ksel = {1'b0, p_hi} + {1'b0, carry_n};\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm = (ksel == 2'd0) ? em0 : (ksel == 2'd1) ? em1 : em2;\n  wire [4:0] exp_o    = is_sub ? {4'd0, carry_s} : exp_norm;\n  wire       ovf      = ovf_cmp | (t_eq45 & carry_n);\n\n  // ---- flattened packing ----\n  wire not_special = ~nan_in & ~inf_in & ~zero_in;\n  wire gate_f      = not_special & ~ovf;\n\n  wire [9:0] frac_y;\n  assign frac_y[8:0] = frac_o[8:0] & {9{gate_f}};\n  assign frac_y[9]   = nan_in | (frac_o[9] & gate_f);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y = (nan_in | inf_in) ? 5'd31 :\n                     zero_in ? 5'd0 :\n                     ovf ? 5'd31 : exp_o;\n\n  wire sign_y = sy & ~nan_in;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='v14.v')

[ASSISTANT]
The v14 critical path ends at exponent bit y[10], which depends on `carry_n` through a 9-level ripple prefix-AND. Let me create v16 with balanced (Kogge-Stone) parallel-prefix for the rounding increment on both paths, computed before the p_hi mux so it overlaps the multiplier:


  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v16 -- v14 (early AND-OR subnormal alignment) with balanced\n// Kogge-Stone parallel-prefix AND for the rounding increment. Prefixes of both\n// normal candidates (p_hi=0/1) are computed in parallel (before the p_hi mux),\n// so carry is ~4 levels instead of a 9-level ripple.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // parallel-prefix AND: out[i] = &in[i:0]  (Kogge-Stone, 10-bit, 4 stages)\n  function automatic [9:0] pfx_and10;\n    input [9:0] x;\n    reg [9:0] g;\n    integer i;\n    begin\n      g = x;\n      for (i=9;i>=1;i=i-1) g[i] = g[i] & g[i-1];   // d=1\n      for (i=9;i>=2;i=i-1) g[i] = g[i] & g[i-2];   // d=2\n      for (i=9;i>=4;i=i-1) g[i] = g[i] & g[i-4];   // d=4\n      for (i=9;i>=8;i=i-1) g[i] = g[i] & g[i-8];   // d=8\n      pfx_and10 = g;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // ---- unpack ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire ez_a = ~|ea, ez_b = ~|eb;\n  wire eo_a = &ea,  eo_b = &eb;\n  wire fz_a = ~|fa, fz_b = ~|fb;\n  wire zero_a = ez_a & fz_a,  zero_b = ez_b & fz_b;\n  wire inf_a  = eo_a & fz_a,  inf_b  = eo_b & fz_b;\n  wire nan_a  = eo_a & ~fz_a, nan_b  = eo_b & ~fz_b;\n  wire sub_a  = ez_a & ~fz_a, sub_b  = ez_b & ~fz_b;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count of the 10-bit fractions ----\n  wire [3:0] lz_a = fa[9] ? 4'd0 : fa[8] ? 4'd1 : fa[7] ? 4'd2 : fa[6] ? 4'd3 :\n                    fa[5] ? 4'd4 : fa[4] ? 4'd5 : fa[3] ? 4'd6 : fa[2] ? 4'd7 :\n                    fa[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n  wire [3:0] lz_b = fb[9] ? 4'd0 : fb[8] ? 4'd1 : fb[7] ? 4'd2 : fb[6] ? 4'd3 :\n                    fb[5] ? 4'd4 : fb[4] ? 4'd5 : fb[3] ? 4'd6 : fb[2] ? 4'd7 :\n                    fb[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n\n  // ---- normalized significands (leading 1 at bit 10) ----\n  wire [10:0] ma = sub_a ? ({fa, 1'b0} << lz_a) : {1'b1, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb = sub_b ? ({fb, 1'b0} << lz_b) : {1'b1, fb};\n\n  // ---- exponent sum (early) ----\n  wire signed [6:0] ea_e = sub_a ? -$signed({3'b000, lz_a}) : $signed({2'b00, ea});\n  wire signed [6:0] eb_e = sub_b ? -$signed({3'b000, lz_b}) : $signed({2'b00, eb});\n  wire signed [6:0] t_base = ea_e + eb_e;\n\n  wire tb_le14 = (t_base <= 7'sd14);\n  wire tb_le15 = (t_base <= 7'sd15);\n  wire tb_ge45 = (t_base >= 7'sd45);\n  wire tb_ge46 = (t_base >= 7'sd46);\n  wire tb_eq44 = (t_base == 7'sd44);\n  wire tb_eq45 = (t_base == 7'sd45);\n\n  wire [4:0] em0 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd15;\n  wire [4:0] em1 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd14;\n  wire [4:0] em2 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd13;\n\n  wire [3:0] R = (t_base >= 7'sd0) ? (4'd15 - t_base[3:0]) : 4'd15;\n\n  // ---- one-hot decode of R (early) ----\n  wire [15:0] Req;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 16; k = k + 1)\n      assign Req[k] = (R == k[3:0]);\n  endgenerate\n\n  // ---- multiply ----\n  wire [21:0] p    = ma * mb;\n  wire        p_hi = p[21];\n\n  wire is_sub  = p_hi ? tb_le14 : tb_le15;\n  wire ovf_cmp = p_hi ? tb_ge45 : tb_ge46;\n  wire t_eq45  = p_hi ? tb_eq44 : tb_eq45;\n\n  // ---- prefix OR of p for sticky ----\n  wire [21:0] pfx;\n  assign pfx[0] = p[0];\n  genvar q;\n  generate\n    for (q = 1; q < 22; q = q + 1) assign pfx[q] = pfx[q-1] | p[q];\n  endgenerate\n\n  // ================= subnormal path: AND-OR plane =================\n  wire [9:0] frac_s;\n  genvar i;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 10; i = i + 1) begin : gfs\n      wire [11:0] t;\n      genvar kk;\n      for (kk = 0; kk < 12; kk = kk + 1) begin : gt\n        if (11 + kk + i <= 21)\n          assign t[kk] = Req[kk] & p[11+kk+i];\n        else\n          assign t[kk] = 1'b0;\n      end\n      assign frac_s[i] = |t;\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [11:0] gt;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 12; k = k + 1)\n      assign gt[k] = Req[k] & p[10+k];\n  endgenerate\n  wire guard_s = |gt;\n\n  wire [15:0] st;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 16; k = k + 1) begin : gst\n      assign st[k] = Req[k] & ((9+k <= 21) ? pfx[9+k] : pfx[21]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  wire sticky_s = |st;\n\n  wire round_s = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_s[0]);\n\n  wire [9:0] pa_s = pfx_and10(frac_s);\n  wire [9:0] frac_s_o;\n  assign frac_s_o[0] = frac_s[0] ^ round_s;\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1)\n      assign frac_s_o[i] = frac_s[i] ^ (round_s & pa_s[i-1]);\n  endgenerate\n  wire carry_s = round_s & pa_s[9];\n\n  // ================= normal rounding path =================\n  // compute both p_hi candidates' fields + prefixes in parallel\n  wire [9:0] frac_na = p[19:10];       // p_hi = 0\n  wire [9:0] frac_nb = p[20:11];       // p_hi = 1\n  wire       guard_na = p[9];\n  wire       guard_nb = p[10];\n  wire       sticky_na = pfx[8];\n  wire       sticky_nb = pfx[9];\n  wire       round_na = guard_na & (sticky_na | frac_na[0]);\n  wire       round_nb = guard_nb & (sticky_nb | frac_nb[0]);\n\n  wire [9:0] pa_na = pfx_and10(frac_na);\n  wire [9:0] pa_nb = pfx_and10(frac_nb);\n\n  wire [9:0] frac_n_o_a, frac_n_o_b;\n  assign frac_n_o_a[0] = frac_na[0] ^ round_na;\n  assign frac_n_o_b[0] = frac_nb[0] ^ round_nb;\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1) begin : gn\n      assign frac_n_o_a[i] = frac_na[i] ^ (round_na & pa_na[i-1]);\n      assign frac_n_o_b[i] = frac_nb[i] ^ (round_nb & pa_nb[i-1]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  wire carry_na = round_na & pa_na[9];\n  wire carry_nb = round_nb & pa_nb[9];\n\n  wire [9:0] frac_n_o = p_hi ? frac_n_o_b : frac_n_o_a;\n  wire       carry_n  = p_hi ? carry_nb   : carry_na;\n\n  // ================= merge =================\n  wire [9:0] frac_o = is_sub ? frac_s_o : frac_n_o;\n\n  // ---- final exponent ----\n  wire [1:0] ksel = {1'b0, p_hi} + {1'b0, carry_n};\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm = (ksel == 2'd0) ? em0 : (ksel == 2'd1) ? em1 : em2;\n  wire [4:0] exp_o    = is_sub ? {4'd0, carry_s} : exp_norm;\n  wire       ovf      = ovf_cmp | (t_eq45 & carry_n);\n\n  // ---- flattened packing ----\n  wire not_special = ~nan_in & ~inf_in & ~zero_in;\n  wire gate_f      = not_special & ~ovf;\n\n  wire [9:0] frac_y;\n  assign frac_y[8:0] = frac_o[8:0] & {9{gate_f}};\n  assign frac_y[9]   = nan_in | (frac_o[9] & gate_f);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y = (nan_in | inf_in) ? 5'd31 :\n                     zero_in ? 5'd0 :\n                     ovf ? 5'd31 : exp_o;\n\n  wire sign_y = sy & ~nan_in;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='v16.v')

[ASSISTANT]
I realized a key carry simplification: `carry = round & &frac`, but `round = guard & (sticky | frac[0])`. When `&frac=1`, `frac[0]=1`, so `(sticky|frac[0])=1`, giving **`carry = guard & (&frac)`** — dropping the sticky dependency from the carry. Let me apply to both paths:


  -> edit_file(filename='v14.v', new_string='  // fast carry: carry = round & (&frac); since (&frac) implies frac[0]=1,\n  // round = guard&(sticky|frac0) collapses to guard -> carry = guard & (&frac)\n  wire carry_s = guard_s & (&frac_s);', old_string='  // fast balanced AND for the carry (exponent path), independent of ripple pa\n  wire carry_s = round_s & (&frac_s);')

[ASSISTANT]
Now let me try v17: restructure the exponent output to remove two mux levels. Key changes: `exp_norm = em0 + {p_hi, carry_n}` (one small late adder instead of ksel+3:1 mux), and flat AND-OR exponent outputs folding in the is_sub/zero/force31 selects:


  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v17 -- v14 with a flattened exponent output.\n// exp_norm = em0 + p_hi + carry_n (one small late adder, no ksel mux), and\n// exp_y is a flat AND-OR plane (force31 / normal / subnormal-carry terms),\n// removing the exp_o and exp_y mux chains from the critical path.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- unpack ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire ez_a = ~|ea, ez_b = ~|eb;\n  wire eo_a = &ea,  eo_b = &eb;\n  wire fz_a = ~|fa, fz_b = ~|fb;\n  wire zero_a = ez_a & fz_a,  zero_b = ez_b & fz_b;\n  wire inf_a  = eo_a & fz_a,  inf_b  = eo_b & fz_b;\n  wire nan_a  = eo_a & ~fz_a, nan_b  = eo_b & ~fz_b;\n  wire sub_a  = ez_a & ~fz_a, sub_b  = ez_b & ~fz_b;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count of the 10-bit fractions ----\n  wire [3:0] lz_a = fa[9] ? 4'd0 : fa[8] ? 4'd1 : fa[7] ? 4'd2 : fa[6] ? 4'd3 :\n                    fa[5] ? 4'd4 : fa[4] ? 4'd5 : fa[3] ? 4'd6 : fa[2] ? 4'd7 :\n                    fa[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n  wire [3:0] lz_b = fb[9] ? 4'd0 : fb[8] ? 4'd1 : fb[7] ? 4'd2 : fb[6] ? 4'd3 :\n                    fb[5] ? 4'd4 : fb[4] ? 4'd5 : fb[3] ? 4'd6 : fb[2] ? 4'd7 :\n                    fb[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n\n  // ---- normalized significands (leading 1 at bit 10) ----\n  wire [10:0] ma = sub_a ? ({fa, 1'b0} << lz_a) : {1'b1, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb = sub_b ? ({fb, 1'b0} << lz_b) : {1'b1, fb};\n\n  // ---- exponent sum (early) ----\n  wire signed [6:0] ea_e = sub_a ? -$signed({3'b000, lz_a}) : $signed({2'b00, ea});\n  wire signed [6:0] eb_e = sub_b ? -$signed({3'b000, lz_b}) : $signed({2'b00, eb});\n  wire signed [6:0] t_base = ea_e + eb_e;\n\n  wire tb_le14 = (t_base <= 7'sd14);\n  wire tb_le15 = (t_base <= 7'sd15);\n  wire tb_ge45 = (t_base >= 7'sd45);\n  wire tb_ge46 = (t_base >= 7'sd46);\n  wire tb_eq44 = (t_base == 7'sd44);\n  wire tb_eq45 = (t_base == 7'sd45);\n\n  wire [4:0] em0 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd15;\n\n  wire [3:0] R = (t_base >= 7'sd0) ? (4'd15 - t_base[3:0]) : 4'd15;\n\n  // ---- one-hot decode of R (early) ----\n  wire [15:0] Req;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 16; k = k + 1)\n      assign Req[k] = (R == k[3:0]);\n  endgenerate\n\n  // ---- multiply ----\n  wire [21:0] p    = ma * mb;\n  wire        p_hi = p[21];\n\n  wire is_sub  = p_hi ? tb_le14 : tb_le15;\n  wire ovf_cmp = p_hi ? tb_ge45 : tb_ge46;\n  wire t_eq45  = p_hi ? tb_eq44 : tb_eq45;\n\n  // ---- prefix OR of p for sticky ----\n  wire [21:0] pfx;\n  assign pfx[0] = p[0];\n  genvar q;\n  generate\n    for (q = 1; q < 22; q = q + 1) assign pfx[q] = pfx[q-1] | p[q];\n  endgenerate\n\n  // ================= subnormal path: AND-OR plane =================\n  wire [9:0] frac_s;\n  genvar i;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 10; i = i + 1) begin : gfs\n      wire [11:0] t;\n      genvar kk;\n      for (kk = 0; kk < 12; kk = kk + 1) begin : gt\n        if (11 + kk + i <= 21)\n          assign t[kk] = Req[kk] & p[11+kk+i];\n        else\n          assign t[kk] = 1'b0;\n      end\n      assign frac_s[i] = |t;\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [11:0] gt;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 12; k = k + 1)\n      assign gt[k] = Req[k] & p[10+k];\n  endgenerate\n  wire guard_s = |gt;\n\n  wire [15:0] st;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 16; k = k + 1) begin : gst\n      assign st[k] = Req[k] & ((9+k <= 21) ? pfx[9+k] : pfx[21]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  wire sticky_s = |st;\n\n  wire round_s = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_s[0]);\n\n  wire [9:0] pa_s;\n  assign pa_s[0] = frac_s[0];\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1) assign pa_s[i] = pa_s[i-1] & frac_s[i];\n  endgenerate\n  wire [9:0] frac_s_o;\n  assign frac_s_o[0] = frac_s[0] ^ round_s;\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1)\n      assign frac_s_o[i] = frac_s[i] ^ (round_s & pa_s[i-1]);\n  endgenerate\n  wire carry_s = round_s & pa_s[9];\n\n  // ================= normal rounding path =================\n  wire [9:0] frac_n  = p_hi ? p[20:11] : p[19:10];\n  wire       guard_n = p_hi ? p[10]    : p[9];\n  wire       sticky_n = p_hi ? pfx[9]  : pfx[8];\n  wire       round_n = guard_n & (sticky_n | frac_n[0]);\n\n  wire [9:0] pa_n;\n  assign pa_n[0] = frac_n[0];\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1) assign pa_n[i] = pa_n[i-1] & frac_n[i];\n  endgenerate\n  wire [9:0] frac_n_o;\n  assign frac_n_o[0] = frac_n[0] ^ round_n;\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1)\n      assign frac_n_o[i] = frac_n[i] ^ (round_n & pa_n[i-1]);\n  endgenerate\n  wire carry_n = round_n & pa_n[9];\n\n  // ---- overflow (late) ----\n  wire ovf = ovf_cmp | (t_eq45 & carry_n);\n\n  // ---- final exponent: flat AND-OR ----\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm = em0 + {3'd0, p_hi, carry_n};\n  wire nif      = nan_in | inf_in;\n  wire force31  = nif | ovf;\n  wire core_en  = ~zero_in & ~force31;\n  wire nor_en   = core_en & ~is_sub;\n  wire sub_en   = core_en & is_sub;\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y;\n  assign exp_y[0] = force31 | (nor_en & exp_norm[0]) | (sub_en & carry_s);\n  genvar e;\n  generate\n    for (e = 1; e < 5; e = e + 1)\n      assign exp_y[e] = force31 | (nor_en & exp_norm[e]);\n  endgenerate\n\n  // ---- flattened fraction packing ----\n  wire [9:0] frac_o = is_sub ? frac_s_o : frac_n_o;\n  wire gate_f = core_en & ~ovf;\n\n  wire [9:0] frac_y;\n  assign frac_y[8:0] = frac_o[8:0] & {9{gate_f}};\n  assign frac_y[9]   = nan_in | (frac_o[9] & gate_f);\n\n  wire sign_y = sy & ~nan_in;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='v17.v')

[ASSISTANT]
v17 has a bug: `{3'd0, p_hi, carry_n}` is a **concatenation** (= p_hi·2 + carry_n), but I need the arithmetic **sum** (p_hi + carry_n). When p_hi=1,carry_n=0 it gives 2 instead of 1 → the +1 exponent error. Let me fix it:


  -> edit_file(filename='v17.v', new_string="  // ---- final exponent: flat AND-OR ----\n  wire [1:0] ksel = {1'b0, p_hi} + {1'b0, carry_n};   // arithmetic sum 0..2\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm = em0 + {3'd0, ksel};", old_string="  // ---- final exponent: flat AND-OR ----\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm = em0 + {3'd0, p_hi, carry_n};")

[ASSISTANT]
v17 (103132) is worse than v14 (100947, best). Let me make targeted improvements to v14: (1) the sticky prefix-OR `pfx` is a **linear 21-level ripple** — replace with balanced prefix OR; (2) compute carries with balanced AND trees directly from `p`, bypassing the 9-level ripple for the exponent path. Creating v18:


  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v18 -- v14 with balanced prefix OR (sticky) and balanced carry\n// computation: pfx uses Kogge-Stone prefix OR (5 levels instead of 21-level\n// ripple); carries use &frac computed straight from p (bypassing the ripple).\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // balanced prefix OR: out[i] = |in[i:0] (Kogge-Stone, 22-bit, 5 stages)\n  function automatic [21:0] pfx_or22;\n    input [21:0] x;\n    reg [21:0] g;\n    integer i;\n    begin\n      g = x;\n      for (i=21;i>=1;i=i-1) g[i] = g[i] | g[i-1];    // d=1\n      for (i=21;i>=2;i=i-1) g[i] = g[i] | g[i-2];    // d=2\n      for (i=21;i>=4;i=i-1) g[i] = g[i] | g[i-4];    // d=4\n      for (i=21;i>=8;i=i-1) g[i] = g[i] | g[i-8];    // d=8\n      for (i=21;i>=16;i=i-1) g[i] = g[i] | g[i-16];  // d=16\n      pfx_or22 = g;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // ---- unpack ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire ez_a = ~|ea, ez_b = ~|eb;\n  wire eo_a = &ea,  eo_b = &eb;\n  wire fz_a = ~|fa, fz_b = ~|fb;\n  wire zero_a = ez_a & fz_a,  zero_b = ez_b & fz_b;\n  wire inf_a  = eo_a & fz_a,  inf_b  = eo_b & fz_b;\n  wire nan_a  = eo_a & ~fz_a, nan_b  = eo_b & ~fz_b;\n  wire sub_a  = ez_a & ~fz_a, sub_b  = ez_b & ~fz_b;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count of the 10-bit fractions ----\n  wire [3:0] lz_a = fa[9] ? 4'd0 : fa[8] ? 4'd1 : fa[7] ? 4'd2 : fa[6] ? 4'd3 :\n                    fa[5] ? 4'd4 : fa[4] ? 4'd5 : fa[3] ? 4'd6 : fa[2] ? 4'd7 :\n                    fa[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n  wire [3:0] lz_b = fb[9] ? 4'd0 : fb[8] ? 4'd1 : fb[7] ? 4'd2 : fb[6] ? 4'd3 :\n                    fb[5] ? 4'd4 : fb[4] ? 4'd5 : fb[3] ? 4'd6 : fb[2] ? 4'd7 :\n                    fb[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n\n  // ---- normalized significands (leading 1 at bit 10) ----\n  wire [10:0] ma = sub_a ? ({fa, 1'b0} << lz_a) : {1'b1, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb = sub_b ? ({fb, 1'b0} << lz_b) : {1'b1, fb};\n\n  // ---- exponent sum (early) ----\n  wire signed [6:0] ea_e = sub_a ? -$signed({3'b000, lz_a}) : $signed({2'b00, ea});\n  wire signed [6:0] eb_e = sub_b ? -$signed({3'b000, lz_b}) : $signed({2'b00, eb});\n  wire signed [6:0] t_base = ea_e + eb_e;\n\n  wire tb_le14 = (t_base <= 7'sd14);\n  wire tb_le15 = (t_base <= 7'sd15);\n  wire tb_ge45 = (t_base >= 7'sd45);\n  wire tb_ge46 = (t_base >= 7'sd46);\n  wire tb_eq44 = (t_base == 7'sd44);\n  wire tb_eq45 = (t_base == 7'sd45);\n\n  wire [4:0] em0 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd15;\n  wire [4:0] em1 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd14;\n  wire [4:0] em2 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd13;\n\n  wire [3:0] R = (t_base >= 7'sd0) ? (4'd15 - t_base[3:0]) : 4'd15;\n\n  // ---- one-hot decode of R (early) ----\n  wire [15:0] Req;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 16; k = k + 1)\n      assign Req[k] = (R == k[3:0]);\n  endgenerate\n\n  // ---- multiply ----\n  wire [21:0] p    = ma * mb;\n  wire        p_hi = p[21];\n\n  wire is_sub  = p_hi ? tb_le14 : tb_le15;\n  wire ovf_cmp = p_hi ? tb_ge45 : tb_ge46;\n  wire t_eq45  = p_hi ? tb_eq44 : tb_eq45;\n\n  // ---- balanced prefix OR of p for sticky ----\n  wire [21:0] pfx = pfx_or22(p);\n\n  // ================= subnormal path: AND-OR plane =================\n  wire [9:0] frac_s;\n  genvar i;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 10; i = i + 1) begin : gfs\n      wire [11:0] t;\n      genvar kk;\n      for (kk = 0; kk < 12; kk = kk + 1) begin : gt\n        if (11 + kk + i <= 21)\n          assign t[kk] = Req[kk] & p[11+kk+i];\n        else\n          assign t[kk] = 1'b0;\n      end\n      assign frac_s[i] = |t;\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [11:0] gt;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 12; k = k + 1)\n      assign gt[k] = Req[k] & p[10+k];\n  endgenerate\n  wire guard_s = |gt;\n\n  // sticky_s = OR_k Req[k] & pfx[9+k];  k=0..11 valid; k>=12 -> pfx[21]\n  wire [11:0] st;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 12; k = k + 1)\n      assign st[k] = Req[k] & pfx[9+k];\n  endgenerate\n  wire sticky_s = (|st) | ((Req[12]|Req[13]|Req[14]|Req[15]) & pfx[21]);\n\n  wire round_s = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_s[0]);\n\n  // ripple prefixes for the rounded fraction bits (needed for XOR outputs)\n  wire [9:0] pa_s;\n  assign pa_s[0] = frac_s[0];\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1) assign pa_s[i] = pa_s[i-1] & frac_s[i];\n  endgenerate\n  wire [9:0] frac_s_o;\n  assign frac_s_o[0] = frac_s[0] ^ round_s;\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1)\n      assign frac_s_o[i] = frac_s[i] ^ (round_s & pa_s[i-1]);\n  endgenerate\n  // balanced carry for the exponent path (&frac_s = pa_s[9], 10-bit AND tree)\n  wire carry_s = round_s & (&frac_s);\n\n  // ================= normal rounding path =================\n  wire [20:0] mant = p_hi ? p[21:1] : p[20:0];\n  wire [9:0] frac_n  = mant[19:10];\n  wire       guard_n = mant[9];\n  wire       sticky_n = (|mant[8:0]) | (p_hi & p[0]);\n  wire       round_n = guard_n & (sticky_n | frac_n[0]);\n\n  wire [9:0] pa_n;\n  assign pa_n[0] = frac_n[0];\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1) assign pa_n[i] = pa_n[i-1] & frac_n[i];\n  endgenerate\n  wire [9:0] frac_n_o;\n  assign frac_n_o[0] = frac_n[0] ^ round_n;\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1)\n      assign frac_n_o[i] = frac_n[i] ^ (round_n & pa_n[i-1]);\n  endgenerate\n  // balanced carry straight from p (no mux, no ripple):\n  // (&frac_n) = p_hi ? (&p[20:11]) : (&p[19:10])\n  wire carry_n = round_n & (p_hi ? (&p[20:11]) : (&p[19:10]));\n\n  // ================= merge =================\n  wire [9:0] frac_o = is_sub ? frac_s_o : frac_n_o;\n\n  // ---- final exponent ----\n  wire [1:0] ksel = {1'b0, p_hi} + {1'b0, carry_n};\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm = (ksel == 2'd0) ? em0 : (ksel == 2'd1) ? em1 : em2;\n  wire [4:0] exp_o    = is_sub ? {4'd0, carry_s} : exp_norm;\n  wire       ovf      = ovf_cmp | (t_eq45 & carry_n);\n\n  // ---- flattened packing ----\n  wire not_special = ~nan_in & ~inf_in & ~zero_in;\n  wire gate_f      = not_special & ~ovf;\n\n  wire [9:0] frac_y;\n  assign frac_y[8:0] = frac_o[8:0] & {9{gate_f}};\n  assign frac_y[9]   = nan_in | (frac_o[9] & gate_f);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y = (nan_in | inf_in) ? 5'd31 :\n                     zero_in ? 5'd0 :\n                     ovf ? 5'd31 : exp_o;\n\n  wire sign_y = sy & ~nan_in;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='v18.v')

v18 (103502) worse than v14 (100947). The `&frac` tree and Kogge-Stone ORs don't map well. Key insight: v14's critical path ends at **y[10] = exponent bit 0**, going through the exp_o mux + exp_y 3-level mux chain. v17's flattening idea was right but its `em0+ksel` adder was the problem. Let me try v19: v14 + **only** flat exponent output (keeping the fast em0/em1/em2 mux):


  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v19 -- v14 with a flat AND-OR exponent output.\n// exp_norm keeps the em0/em1/em2 mux (fed by ksel), but the exp_o/exp_y mux\n// chain (is_sub / ovf / zero / nan-inf) is replaced by a flat AND-OR plane.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- unpack ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire ez_a = ~|ea, ez_b = ~|eb;\n  wire eo_a = &ea,  eo_b = &eb;\n  wire fz_a = ~|fa, fz_b = ~|fb;\n  wire zero_a = ez_a & fz_a,  zero_b = ez_b & fz_b;\n  wire inf_a  = eo_a & fz_a,  inf_b  = eo_b & fz_b;\n  wire nan_a  = eo_a & ~fz_a, nan_b  = eo_b & ~fz_b;\n  wire sub_a  = ez_a & ~fz_a, sub_b  = ez_b & ~fz_b;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count of the 10-bit fractions ----\n  wire [3:0] lz_a = fa[9] ? 4'd0 : fa[8] ? 4'd1 : fa[7] ? 4'd2 : fa[6] ? 4'd3 :\n                    fa[5] ? 4'd4 : fa[4] ? 4'd5 : fa[3] ? 4'd6 : fa[2] ? 4'd7 :\n                    fa[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n  wire [3:0] lz_b = fb[9] ? 4'd0 : fb[8] ? 4'd1 : fb[7] ? 4'd2 : fb[6] ? 4'd3 :\n                    fb[5] ? 4'd4 : fb[4] ? 4'd5 : fb[3] ? 4'd6 : fb[2] ? 4'd7 :\n                    fb[1] ? 4'd8 : 4'd9;\n\n  // ---- normalized significands (leading 1 at bit 10) ----\n  wire [10:0] ma = sub_a ? ({fa, 1'b0} << lz_a) : {1'b1, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb = sub_b ? ({fb, 1'b0} << lz_b) : {1'b1, fb};\n\n  // ---- exponent sum (early) ----\n  wire signed [6:0] ea_e = sub_a ? -$signed({3'b000, lz_a}) : $signed({2'b00, ea});\n  wire signed [6:0] eb_e = sub_b ? -$signed({3'b000, lz_b}) : $signed({2'b00, eb});\n  wire signed [6:0] t_base = ea_e + eb_e;\n\n  wire tb_le14 = (t_base <= 7'sd14);\n  wire tb_le15 = (t_base <= 7'sd15);\n  wire tb_ge45 = (t_base >= 7'sd45);\n  wire tb_ge46 = (t_base >= 7'sd46);\n  wire tb_eq44 = (t_base == 7'sd44);\n  wire tb_eq45 = (t_base == 7'sd45);\n\n  wire [4:0] em0 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd15;\n  wire [4:0] em1 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd14;\n  wire [4:0] em2 = t_base[4:0] - 5'd13;\n\n  wire [3:0] R = (t_base >= 7'sd0) ? (4'd15 - t_base[3:0]) : 4'd15;\n\n  // ---- one-hot decode of R (early) ----\n  wire [15:0] Req;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 16; k = k + 1)\n      assign Req[k] = (R == k[3:0]);\n  endgenerate\n\n  // ---- multiply ----\n  wire [21:0] p    = ma * mb;\n  wire        p_hi = p[21];\n\n  wire is_sub  = p_hi ? tb_le14 : tb_le15;\n  wire ovf_cmp = p_hi ? tb_ge45 : tb_ge46;\n  wire t_eq45  = p_hi ? tb_eq44 : tb_eq45;\n\n  // ---- prefix OR of p for sticky ----\n  wire [21:0] pfx;\n  assign pfx[0] = p[0];\n  genvar q;\n  generate\n    for (q = 1; q < 22; q = q + 1) assign pfx[q] = pfx[q-1] | p[q];\n  endgenerate\n\n  // ================= subnormal path: AND-OR plane =================\n  wire [9:0] frac_s;\n  genvar i;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 10; i = i + 1) begin : gfs\n      wire [11:0] t;\n      genvar kk;\n      for (kk = 0; kk < 12; kk = kk + 1) begin : gt\n        if (11 + kk + i <= 21)\n          assign t[kk] = Req[kk] & p[11+kk+i];\n        else\n          assign t[kk] = 1'b0;\n      end\n      assign frac_s[i] = |t;\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [11:0] gt;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 12; k = k + 1)\n      assign gt[k] = Req[k] & p[10+k];\n  endgenerate\n  wire guard_s = |gt;\n\n  wire [15:0] st;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 16; k = k + 1) begin : gst\n      assign st[k] = Req[k] & ((9+k <= 21) ? pfx[9+k] : pfx[21]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  wire sticky_s = |st;\n\n  wire round_s = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_s[0]);\n\n  wire [9:0] pa_s;\n  assign pa_s[0] = frac_s[0];\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1) assign pa_s[i] = pa_s[i-1] & frac_s[i];\n  endgenerate\n  wire [9:0] frac_s_o;\n  assign frac_s_o[0] = frac_s[0] ^ round_s;\n  generate\n    for (i = 1; i < 10; i = i + 1)\n      assign frac_s_o[i] = frac_s[i] ^ (round_s & pa_s[i-1]);\n  endgenerate\n  wire carry_s = round_s & pa_s[9];\n\n  // ================= normal rounding path =================\n  wire [20:0] mant = p_hi ? v14 improved to **100495.7** (new best). One more optimization: `sticky_s` includes `st[12..15]` terms that use `pfx[21]` (slowest ripple bit), but they're redundant — when R≥12, `guard_s=0` forces `round_s=0` regardless. Removing them speeds up `round_s`:


  -> edit_file(filename='v14.v', new_string='  // sticky_s = OR_k Req[k] & pfx[9+k];  only k <= 11 matter (k >= 12 has\n  // guard_s = 0, so round_s = 0 regardless of sticky)\n  wire [11:0] st;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 12; k = k + 1) begin : gst\n      assign st[k] = Req[k] & pfx[9+k];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  wire sticky_s = |st;', old_string='  wire [15:0] st;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 16; k = k + 1) begin : gst\n      assign st[k] = Req[k] & ((9+k <= 21) ? pfx[9+k] : pfx[21]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  wire sticky_s = |st;')

## Summary

**Final: v14.v — PASS, cost 99,259 area_delay_product** (baseline starting_point.v: 145,429 → **32% reduction**; area 92.5, delay 1073 ps).

### 1. Approaches tried; what worked best
The winning design (`v14.v`) combines several restructuring ideas:
- **Input-side subnormal normalization** (v2): normalize subnormal operands (10-bit LZC + left shift) before the 11×11 multiply, so the product has at most one leading zero — eliminating the 22-bit LZC and wide barrel shifter of the starting point.
- **Early exponent arithmetic** (v7): compute `t_base = ea_eff + eb_eff` pre-multiplier; pre-compute all threshold comparisons (`<=14/15`, `>=45/46`, `==44/45`) and exponent tables (`em0/em1/em2`, shift `R`) early, letting the late multiplier MSB (`p_hi`) only do tiny 2:1 selects.
- **Early one-hot decoded AND-OR alignment plane** (v14's key win): since `R = 15 − t_base` is known before the multiplier, the subnormal result fraction/guard/sticky bits are each a single AND-OR level straight off the product bits (12-input OR), replacing the 4-stage funnel shifter + sticky OR chain (~200 ps saved on the critical path).
- **Carry-free rounding increment**: `frac_o[i] = frac[i] ^ (round_up & pa[i-1])` with prefix-ANDs computed from pre-round bits, so the late `round_up` reaches each fraction bit through one XOR.
- **Flattened output packing**: fraction bits gated by `not_special & ~ovf` AND-planes instead of a 3-level nan/inf/zero/core mux chain.

### 2. Most impactful optimizations
1. AND-OR plane subnormal alignment (102,784 → 100,947).
2. One-hot `Req` decode directly from `t_base[3:0]`, skipping the `15−t_base` subtractor (→ 100,496).
3. Dropping redundant `st[12..15]` sticky terms (guard=0 there, so they can never round) — removed the slowest `pfx[21]` ripple bit from the `round_s` path (→ 99,259).
4. The original input-side normalization + early exponent restructuring (145,429 → ~110,000).

### 3. What didn't work / regressed
- **Kogge-Stone parallel-prefix** replacements of the 10-bit prefix-ANDs (v10, v16) and of the 22-bit sticky prefix-OR (v18): slightly worse — Yosys maps the hand-rippled forms better at this width; carry is rarely the bottleneck.
- **Flattening the exponent output** (v12, v17, v19): consistently worse — the `is_sub/ovf/zero` mux tree synthesizes efficiently; the flat AND-OR version grew the exponent adder path.
- `carry = guard & (&frac)` simplification (valid mathematically) made things worse due to the wide AND tree.
- Full AND-OR plane also covering the *normal* path (v13): worse (area 93.3, cost 109,691).
- `target_delay` sweeps had zero effect (synthesis result identical at 300/500/700 ps).
- One bug found via sim: `{3'd0, p_hi, carry_n}` concatenation instead of arithmetic sum in v17 (fixed), and an early sticky bug where `p[0]` was dropped when `p_hi=1` (v2).

### 4. Lessons learned / next time
- **Move late decisions early**: anything computable pre-multiplier (shift amounts, compares, exponent tables) should be; the multiplier output should ideally feed only 1-bit selects and data.
- Replace mux-based shifters with **decoder + AND-OR planes** when the shift control is early-available.
- Verify "equivalent" restructurings with sim, not just hand-tracing — v17's concat bug looked correct on paper.
- Small, targeted micro-edits to the best file (evaluating after each) outperformed wholesale rewrites; every exotic adder/prefix variant was A/B-tested against v14 and reverted if worse.
- Remaining headroom is in the exponent LSB path (`carry_n` ripple → `ksel` mux) and multiplier structure — a next agent could try decomposing the 11×11 multiply (hidden-bit extraction: `2^20 + 2^10·(fa+fb) + fa·fb`) or one-hot input normalization.
